__kernel void ArgImageToBuffer(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __global float*output,__private const int count,
 __read_only image2d_t input_ptr){
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int buffer_4_offset=image_width_idx<<2;
 int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 float4 values=read_imagef(input_ptr,SAMPLER,coord);
 const int remain=count-buffer_4_offset;
 if(remain<4){
 switch(remain){
 case 3:
 output[buffer_4_offset+2]=values.s2;
 case 2:
 output[buffer_4_offset+1]=values.s1;
 case 1:
 output[buffer_4_offset]=values.s0;
}else{
 vstore4(values,0,output+buffer_4_offset);
 if(remain>=4){
 vstore4(values,0,output+buffer_4_offset);
}else if(remain==3){
 int offset=buffer_4_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
 offset++;
 output[offset]=values.y;
 offset++;
 output[offset]=values.z;
}else if(remain==2){
 int offset=buffer_4_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
 offset++;
 output[offset]=values.y;
}else if(remain==1){
 int offset=buffer_4_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
__kernel void ImageToRGBABuffer(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __read_only image2d_t input_ptr,__global uchar*output,
							 __private const int height,__private const int width,__private const int channel_up_4
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 					 __private const float4 scale,__private const float4 bias
 #endif
	int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
	int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
	const int batch_idx 	=image_height_idx/height;
	const int height_idx	=image_height_idx % height;
	const int width_idx 	=image_width_idx % width;
	const int channel_block_idx=image_width_idx/width;
	int buffer_offset=(((batch_idx*height+height_idx)*width+width_idx)*channel_up_4+channel_block_idx)*4;
	int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
	float4 values_f=read_imagef(input_ptr,SAMPLER,coord);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
	values_f=values_f*scale+bias;
#endif
	uchar4 values=convert_uchar4_sat(values_f);
	vstore4(values,0,output+buffer_offset);

 offset+=stride;

}else if(remain_channel==2){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_ptr[offset]=values.x;
 offset+=stride;
 output_ptr[offset]=values.y;
}else if(remain_channel==1){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_ptr[offset]=values.x;

#define GLOBAL_SIZE_1_DIMS __private const int global_size_dim0, 
#define GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __private const int global_size_dim0, __private const int global_size_dim1,
#define GLOBAL_SIZE_3_DIMS \
 __private const int global_size_dim0, __private const int global_size_dim1, __private const int global_size_dim2,
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM1(input1) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0) { \
 return; \
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(input1, input2) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0 || input2 >= global_size_dim1) { \
 return; \
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM3(input1, input2, input3) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0 || input2 >= global_size_dim1 || input3 >= global_size_dim2) { \

#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT(i) \
 out##i = mad(in##i.x, weights0, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.y, weights1, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.z, weights2, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.w, weights3, out##i);
#define CALCULATE_VEC16_OUTPUT(i) \
 out##i = mad(in##i.x, weights.s0123, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.y, weights.s4567, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.z, weights.s89ab, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.w, weights.scdef, out##i);
#define CALCULATE_SLICE_OUTPUT(s_idx) \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in0.x; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in1.x; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in2.x; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in3.x; \

 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in1.y; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in2.y; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in3.y; \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c2_s##s_idx * in0.z; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c2_s##s_idx * in1.z; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c2_s##s_idx * in2.z; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c2_s##s_idx * in3.z; \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in0.w; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in1.w; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in2.w; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in3.w; \
#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT_DOT(i) \
 out##i.x += dot(in##i, weights0); \
 out##i.y += dot(in##i, weights1); \
 out##i.z += dot(in##i, weights2); \
 out##i.w += dot(in##i, weights3);
#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT_WEIGHTS4(i, j) \
 out##i = mad(in##j.x, weights4, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##j.y, weights5, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##j.z, weights6, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##j.w, weights7, out##i);
__kernel void Blob6DConvertFromNCHW(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __write_only image2d_t output,
 __global const float*input_ptr,__private const int dim2,
 __private const int dim3,__private const int dim4,
 __private const int dim5,__private const int channels
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 __global const float*scale,
 __global const float*bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int dim3_idx=image_height_idx % dim3;
 const int batch_dim2_idx=image_height_idx/dim3;
 const int dim2_idx=batch_dim2_idx % dim2;
 const int batch_idx=batch_dim2_idx/dim2;
 const int dim5_idx=image_width_idx % dim5;
 const int channel_updiv_4_dim4_idx=image_width_idx/dim5;
 const int dim4_idx=channel_updiv_4_dim4_idx % dim4;
 const int channel_4_idx=(channel_updiv_4_dim4_idx/dim4)<<2;
 const int buffer_offset=((((batch_idx*channels+channel_4_idx)*dim2+dim2_idx)*
 dim3+dim3_idx)*dim4+dim4_idx)*dim5+dim5_idx;
 const int stride=dim2*dim3*dim4*dim5;
 const int remain_channel=channels-channel_4_idx;
 float4 output_values=0;
 if(remain_channel>=4){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.w=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float4 scale_data=vload4(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float4 bias_data=vload4(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values=output_values*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==3){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float3 scale_data=vload3(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float3 bias_data=vload3(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values.xyz=output_values.xyz*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==2){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float2 scale_data=vload2(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float2 bias_data=vload2(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values.xy=output_values.xy*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==1){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 output_values.x=output_values.x*scale[channel_4_idx]+bias[channel_4_idx];
#endif
 write_imagef(output,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx),
 output_values);

#endif

 __private const int channel_up_4,__write_only image2d_t output){

 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);

 const int height_idx=image_height_idx % output_wh.x;
 const int width_idx=image_width_idx % output_wh.y;
 const int channel_block_idx=image_width_idx/output_wh.y;
 int buffer_offset=
(((batch_idx*channel_up_4+channel_block_idx)*output_wh.x+height_idx)*output_wh.y+width_idx)*4;
 float4 values=vload4(0,input_ptr+buffer_offset);
 int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 write_imagef(output,coord,values);

 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 write_imagef(output,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx),output_values);
__kernel void DWFilterBufferToImage(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __global const float*input_ptr,
 __private const int4 kernel_shape,
 __private const int height_width_size,__write_only image2d_t output){
 const int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 const int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 float4 output_values=0;
 if(kernel_shape.x==1){
 const int input_channel_4_idx=image_height_idx<<2;
 const int buffer_height_idx=image_width_idx/kernel_shape.w;
 const int buffer_width_idx=image_width_idx % kernel_shape.w;
 const int buffer_offset=
 mad24(mad24(input_channel_4_idx,kernel_shape.z,buffer_height_idx),kernel_shape.w,buffer_width_idx);
 const int remain_channel=kernel_shape.y-input_channel_4_idx;
 if(input_channel_4_idx<kernel_shape.y){
 if(remain_channel>=4){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.w=*(input_ptr+offset);
}else if(remain_channel==3){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
}else if(remain_channel==2){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
}else if(remain_channel==1){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 write_imagef(output,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx),output_values);

__kernel void ArgBufferToImage(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __global const float*input_ptr,__private const int count,
 __write_only image2d_t output){
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);

 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int buffer_4_offset=image_width_idx<<2;
 const int remain=count-buffer_4_offset;
 int offset=buffer_4_offset;
 float4 values=0;
 if(remain>=4){
 values=vload4(0,input_ptr+offset);
}else if(remain==3){
 values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset++;
 values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset++;
 values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
}else if(remain==2){
 values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset++;
 values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
}else if(remain==1){
 values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 write_imagef(output,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx),values);
__kernel void RGBABufferToImage(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __global const uchar*input_ptr,__write_only image2d_t output,
								 __private const int height,__private const int width
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
					 		 __private const float4 scale,__private const float4 bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
	const int batch_idx=image_height_idx/height;
	const int height_idx=image_height_idx % height;
	int buffer_offset=((batch_idx*height+height_idx)*width+image_width_idx)*4;
 float4 values=convert_float4(vload4(0,input_ptr+buffer_offset));
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 values=values*scale+bias;
#endif
 int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 write_imagef(output,coord,values);
__kernel void NV21ToImage(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __global const uchar*input_ptr,__write_only image2d_t output,
						 __private const int height,__private const int width
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
					 __private const float4 scale,__private const float4 bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
	int y_offset=image_height_idx*width+image_width_idx;
	int v_offset=width*height+(image_height_idx>>1)*width+(image_width_idx &(~(0x01)));
	int u_offset=v_offset+1;
 int y=(int)(input_ptr[y_offset]);
 int u=(int)(input_ptr[u_offset]);
 int v=(int)(input_ptr[v_offset]);
 u-=128;
 v-=128;
 int r=y+v+((v*103)>>8);
 int g=y-((u*88)>>8)-((v*183)>>8);
 int b=y+u+((u*198)>>8);
 r=clamp(r,0,255);
 g=clamp(g,0,255);
 b=clamp(b,0,255);
 float4 values=(float4)((float)r,(float)g,(float)b,(float)0.0f);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 values=values*scale+bias;
#endif
 int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 write_imagef(output,coord,values);

 dim3+dim3_idx)*dim4+dim4_idx;

 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
}else if(remain_channel==1){

 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 WI_F(output,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx),output_values);

#ifdef MIZAR_SUPPORT_FP16
#pragma OPENCL EXTENSION cl_khr_fp16 : enable
#endif
typedef struct shape_3d { int data[3];} shape_3d;
typedef struct shape_4d { int data[4];} shape_4d;
typedef struct shape_5d { int data[5];} shape_5d;
typedef struct shape_6d { int data[6];} shape_6d;
__constant sampler_t SAMPLER = CLK_NORMALIZED_COORDS_FALSE | CLK_ADDRESS_CLAMP | CLK_FILTER_NEAREST;
#define GLOBAL_SIZE_1_DIMS __private const int global_size_dim0, 
#define GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __private const int global_size_dim0, __private const int global_size_dim1,
#define GLOBAL_SIZE_3_DIMS \
 __private const int global_size_dim0, __private const int global_size_dim1, __private const int global_size_dim2,
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM1(input1) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0) { \
 return; \
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(input1, input2) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0 || input2 >= global_size_dim1) { \
 return; \
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM3(input1, input2, input3) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0 || input2 >= global_size_dim1 || input3 >= global_size_dim2) { \
 return; \
#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT(i) \
 out##i = mad(in##i.x, weights0, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.y, weights1, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.z, weights2, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.w, weights3, out##i);
#define CALCULATE_VEC16_OUTPUT(i) \
 out##i = mad(in##i.x, weights.s0123, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.y, weights.s4567, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.z, weights.s89ab, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.w, weights.scdef, out##i);
#define CALCULATE_SLICE_OUTPUT(s_idx) \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in0.x; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in1.x; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in2.x; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in3.x; \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in0.y; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in1.y; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in2.y; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in3.y; \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c2_s##s_idx * in0.z; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c2_s##s_idx * in1.z; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c2_s##s_idx * in2.z; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c2_s##s_idx * in3.z; \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in0.w; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in1.w; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in2.w; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in3.w; \
#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT_DOT(i) \
 out##i.x += dot(in##i, weights0); \
 out##i.y += dot(in##i, weights1); \
 out##i.z += dot(in##i, weights2); \
 out##i.w += dot(in##i, weights3);
#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT_WEIGHTS4(i, j) \
 out##i = mad(in##j.x, weights4, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##j.y, weights5, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##j.z, weights6, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##j.w, weights7, out##i);
__kernel void ConvertFromNHWC(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __write_only image2d_t output,
 __global const float*input_ptr,
 __private const int height,
 __private const int width,
 __private const int channels
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 __private const float4 scale,
 __private const float4 bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int batch_idx=image_height_idx/height;
 const int height_idx=image_height_idx % height;
 int buffer_offset=
((batch_idx*height+height_idx)*width+image_width_idx)*4;
 const int remain_channel=4-channels;
 float4 output_values=0;
 if(remain_channel==0){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=1;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=1;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=1;
 output_values.w=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 output_values=output_values*scale+bias;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==1){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=1;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=1;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 output_values.x=output_values.x*scale.x+bias.x;
 output_values.y=output_values.y*scale.y+bias.y;
 output_values.z=output_values.z*scale.z+bias.z;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==2){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=1;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 output_values.x=output_values.x*scale.x+bias.x;
 output_values.y=output_values.y*scale.y+bias.y;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==3){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 output_values.x=output_values.x*scale.x+bias.x;
#endif
 write_imagef(output,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx),
 output_values);
__kernel void ConvertFromNCHW(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __write_only image2d_t output,
 __global const float*input_ptr,
 __private const int height,
 __private const int width,
 __private const int channels
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 __global const float*scale,
 __global const float*bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int batch_idx=image_height_idx/height;
 const int height_idx=image_height_idx % height;
 const int width_idx=image_width_idx % width;
 const int channel_4_idx=(image_width_idx/width)<<2;
 const int buffer_offset=
((batch_idx*channels+channel_4_idx)*height+height_idx)*width+
 width_idx;
 const int remain_channel=channels-channel_4_idx;
 const int height_width_size=height*width;
 float4 output_values=0;
 if(remain_channel>=4){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.w=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float4 scale_data=vload4(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float4 bias_data=vload4(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values=output_values*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==3){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float3 scale_data=vload3(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float3 bias_data=vload3(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values.xyz=output_values.xyz*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==2){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float2 scale_data=vload2(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float2 bias_data=vload2(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values.xy=output_values.xy*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==1){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 output_values.x=output_values.x*scale[channel_4_idx]+bias[channel_4_idx];
#endif
 write_imagef(output,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx),
 output_values);
__kernel void ConvertFromN8UC4(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __write_only image2d_t output,
 __global const uchar*input_ptr,
 __private const int height,
 __private const int width
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS

 __private const float4 bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);

 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);

#endif

 write_imagef(output,coord,values);
__kernel void ConvertFromN8UC3(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __write_only image2d_t output,
 __global const uchar*input_ptr,

 __private const float4 scale,

 #endif

 values.y=convert_float(input_ptr[buffer_offset+1]);
 values.z=convert_float(input_ptr[buffer_offset+2]);

 values=values*scale+bias;
#endif
 int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 write_imagef(output,coord,values);

__kernel void ConvertFromNNV21(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __write_only image2d_t output,
 __global const uchar*input_ptr,
 __private const int height,
 __private const int width
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 __private const float4 scale,
 __private const float4 bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 int y_offset=image_height_idx*width+image_width_idx;
 int v_offset=width*height+(image_height_idx>>1)*width+
(image_width_idx &(~(0x01)));
 int u_offset=v_offset+1;
 int y=(int)(input_ptr[y_offset]);
 int u=(int)(input_ptr[u_offset]);
 int v=(int)(input_ptr[v_offset]);
 u-=128;
 v-=128;
 int r=y+v+((v*103)>>8);
 int g=y-((u*88)>>8)-((v*183)>>8);
 int b=y+u+((u*198)>>8);
 r=clamp(r,0,255);
 g=clamp(g,0,255);
 b=clamp(b,0,255);
 float4 values=(float4)((float)r,(float)g,(float)b,(float)0.0f);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 values=values*scale+bias;
#endif
 int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 write_imagef(output,coord,values);
__kernel void ConvertFromN32FC4Image(
 GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __read_only image2d_t input,
 __write_only image2d_t output
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
,__private const float4 scale,
 __private const float4 bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 int iw=get_image_width(input);
 int ih=get_image_height(input);
 float sw=(float)(image_width_idx)/(float)(global_size_dim0);
 float sh=(float)(image_height_idx)/(float)(global_size_dim1);
 int input_w_idx=(int)((float)(iw)*sw);
 int input_h_idx=(int)((float)(ih)*sh);
 int2 coord=(int2)(input_w_idx,input_h_idx);
 int2 coord2=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 float4 values=read_imagef(input,SAMPLER,coord);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 values=values*scale+bias;
#endif
 write_imagef(output,coord2,values);

#endif
typedef struct shape_3d { int data[3];} shape_3d;
typedef struct shape_4d { int data[4];} shape_4d;
typedef struct shape_5d { int data[5];} shape_5d;
typedef struct shape_6d { int data[6];} shape_6d;
__constant sampler_t SAMPLER = CLK_NORMALIZED_COORDS_FALSE | CLK_ADDRESS_CLAMP | CLK_FILTER_NEAREST;
#define GLOBAL_SIZE_1_DIMS __private const int global_size_dim0, 
#define GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __private const int global_size_dim0, __private const int global_size_dim1,
#define GLOBAL_SIZE_3_DIMS \
 __private const int global_size_dim0, __private const int global_size_dim1, __private const int global_size_dim2,
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM1(input1) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0) { \
 return; \
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(input1, input2) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0 || input2 >= global_size_dim1) { \
 return; \
#define DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM3(input1, input2, input3) \
 if (input1 >= global_size_dim0 || input2 >= global_size_dim1 || input3 >= global_size_dim2) { \
 return; \
#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT(i) \
 out##i = mad(in##i.x, weights0, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.y, weights1, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.z, weights2, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.w, weights3, out##i);
#define CALCULATE_VEC16_OUTPUT(i) \

 out##i = mad(in##i.y, weights.s4567, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.z, weights.s89ab, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##i.w, weights.scdef, out##i);
#define CALCULATE_SLICE_OUTPUT(s_idx) \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in0.x; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in1.x; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in2.x; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c0_s##s_idx * in3.x; \
 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in0.y; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in1.y; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in2.y; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c1_s##s_idx * in3.y; \

 out_w0_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in0.w; \
 out_w1_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in1.w; \
 out_w2_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in2.w; \
 out_w3_s##s_idx += weights_c3_s##s_idx * in3.w; \
#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT_DOT(i) \
 out##i.x += dot(in##i, weights0); \
 out##i.y += dot(in##i, weights1); \
 out##i.z += dot(in##i, weights2); \
 out##i.w += dot(in##i, weights3);
#define CALCULATE_OUTPUT_WEIGHTS4(i, j) \
 out##i = mad(in##j.x, weights4, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##j.y, weights5, out##i); \
 out##i = mad(in##j.z, weights6, out##i); \

__kernel void Blob5DConvertFromNCHW(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __write_only image2d_t output,
 __global const float*input_ptr,__private const int dim2,
 __private const int dim3,__private const int dim4,
 __private const int channels
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 __global const float*scale,
 __global const float*bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int dim3_idx=image_height_idx % dim3;
 const int batch_dim2_idx=image_height_idx/dim3;
 const int dim2_idx=batch_dim2_idx % dim2;
 const int batch_idx=batch_dim2_idx/dim2;
 const int dim4_idx=image_width_idx % dim4;
 const int channel_4_idx=(image_width_idx/dim4)<<2;
 const int buffer_offset=(((batch_idx*channels+channel_4_idx)*dim2+dim2_idx)*
 dim3+dim3_idx)*dim4+dim4_idx;
 const int stride=dim2*dim3*dim4;
 const int remain_channel=channels-channel_4_idx;
 float4 output_values=0;
 if(remain_channel>=4){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.w=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float4 scale_data=vload4(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float4 bias_data=vload4(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values=output_values*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==3){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.z=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float3 scale_data=vload3(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float3 bias_data=vload3(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values.xyz=output_values.xyz*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==2){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
 offset+=stride;
 output_values.y=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float2 scale_data=vload2(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float2 bias_data=vload2(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 output_values.xy=output_values.xy*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
}else if(remain_channel==1){
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output_values.x=*(input_ptr+offset);
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 output_values.x=output_values.x*scale[channel_4_idx]+bias[channel_4_idx];
#endif
 write_imagef(output,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx),
 output_values);
#ifdef MIZAR_SUPPORT_FP16
#pragma OPENCL EXTENSION cl_khr_fp16 : enable
#endif

__kernel void ConvertToNCHW(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __read_only image2d_t input_ptr,
 __global float*output,__private const int height,
 __private const int width,
 __private const int channels
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 __global const float*scale,
 __global const float*bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int batch_idx=image_height_idx/height;
 const int height_idx=image_height_idx % height;
 const int width_idx=image_width_idx % width;
 const int channel_4_idx=(image_width_idx/width)<<2;
 int buffer_offset=
((batch_idx*channels+channel_4_idx)*height+height_idx)*width+
 width_idx;
 float4 values=read_imagef(input_ptr,SAMPLER,
(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx));
 const int height_width_size=height*width;
 const int remain_channel=channels-channel_4_idx;
 if(remain_channel>=4){
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float4 scale_data=vload4(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float4 bias_data=vload4(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 values=values*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output[offset]=values.y;
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output[offset]=values.z;
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output[offset]=values.w;
}else if(remain_channel==3){
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float3 scale_data=vload3(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float3 bias_data=vload3(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 values.xyz=values.xyz*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output[offset]=values.y;
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output[offset]=values.z;
}else if(remain_channel==2){
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float2 scale_data=vload2(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float2 bias_data=vload2(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 values.xy=values.xy*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
 offset+=height_width_size;
 output[offset]=values.y;
}else if(remain_channel==1){
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 values.x=values.x*scale[channel_4_idx]+bias[channel_4_idx];
#endif
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
__kernel void ConvertToNCHWC4(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __read_only image2d_t input,
 __global float*output,
 __private const int height,
 __private const int width,
 __private const int channels){
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int batch_idx=image_height_idx/height;
 const int height_idx=image_height_idx % height;
 const int width_idx=image_width_idx % width;
 const int group_idx=image_width_idx/width;
 const int channel_base=group_idx<<2;
 float4 vals=read_imagef(input,SAMPLER,(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx));
 const int groups=(channels+3)>>2;
 int base=(((batch_idx*groups+group_idx)*height+height_idx)*width+width_idx)<<2;
 int remain=channels-channel_base;
 output[base+0]=(remain>0)? vals.x : 0.0f;
 output[base+1]=(remain>1)? vals.y : 0.0f;
 output[base+2]=(remain>2)? vals.z : 0.0f;
 output[base+3]=(remain>3)? vals.w : 0.0f;

 #endif

 channel_block_idx)*4;

#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 values_f=values_f*scale+bias;
#endif

 vstore4(values,0,output+buffer_offset);

 int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 float4 values=read_imagef(input,SAMPLER,coord);

 values=values*scale+bias;
#endif
 write_imagef(output,coord,values);

 channel_block_idx)*

 output[buffer_offset+2]=values.z;

__kernel void Blob5DConvertToNCHW(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __read_only image2d_t input_ptr,
 __global float*output,__private const int dim2,
 __private const int dim3,__private const int dim4,
 __private const int channels
 #ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 __global const float*scale,
 __global const float*bias
 #endif
 int image_width_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int image_height_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 const int dim3_idx=image_height_idx % dim3;
 const int batch_dim2_idx=image_height_idx/dim3;
 const int dim2_idx=batch_dim2_idx % dim2;
 const int batch_idx=batch_dim2_idx/dim2;
 const int dim4_idx=image_width_idx % dim4;
 const int channel_4_idx=(image_width_idx/dim4)<<2;
 const int buffer_offset=(((batch_idx*channels+channel_4_idx)*dim2+dim2_idx)*
 dim3+dim3_idx)*dim4+dim4_idx;
 const int stride=dim2*dim3*dim4;
 int2 coord=(int2)(image_width_idx,image_height_idx);
 float4 values=read_imagef(input_ptr,SAMPLER,coord);
 const int remain_channel=channels-channel_4_idx;
 if(remain_channel>=4){
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float4 scale_data=vload4(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float4 bias_data=vload4(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 values=values*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
 offset+=stride;
 output[offset]=values.y;
 offset+=stride;
 output[offset]=values.z;
 offset+=stride;
 output[offset]=values.w;
}else if(remain_channel==3){
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float3 scale_data=vload3(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float3 bias_data=vload3(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 values.xyz=values.xyz*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
 offset+=stride;
 output[offset]=values.y;
 offset+=stride;
 output[offset]=values.z;
}else if(remain_channel==2){
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 float2 scale_data=vload2(0,scale+channel_4_idx);
 float2 bias_data=vload2(0,bias+channel_4_idx);
 values.xy=values.xy*scale_data+bias_data;
#endif
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
 offset+=stride;
 output[offset]=values.y;
}else if(remain_channel==1){
#ifdef ENABLE_SCALE_BIAS
 values.x=values.x*scale[channel_4_idx]+bias[channel_4_idx];
#endif
 int offset=buffer_offset;
 output[offset]=values.x;
}#ifdef MIZAR_SUPPORT_FP16
#pragma OPENCL EXTENSION cl_khr_fp16 : enable
#endif

__kernel void Conv2DFilterBufferToBuffer(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __global const float*input_ptr,
 __private const int output_channel,__private const int input_channel,__private const int2 kernel_hw,
 __private const int h_w_size,__global FLOAT*output_ptr){
 int oc_idx=get_global_id(0);
 int ic_h_w_idx=get_global_id(1);
 DEAL_NON_UNIFORM_DIM2(oc_idx,ic_h_w_idx);

 const int h_w_idx=ic_h_w_idx % h_w_size;
 const int h_idx=h_w_idx/kernel_hw.y;
 const int w_idx=h_w_idx % kernel_hw.y;
 const int ic_size=global_size_dim1/h_w_size;
 float val=0;
 if(oc_idx<output_channel && ic_idx<input_channel){
 const int input_offset=oc_idx*input_channel*h_w_size+ic_idx*h_w_size+
 h_idx*kernel_hw.y+w_idx;
 val=input_ptr[input_offset];
 const int ocb_idx=oc_idx>>2;
 const int icb_idx=ic_idx>>2;

 output_ptr[output_offset]=(FLOAT)(val);
__kernel void ArgBufferToBuffer(GLOBAL_SIZE_2_DIMS __global const float*input_ptr,__private const int count,
 __global FLOAT*output_ptr){

#ifndef NO_KEEPDIMS
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
#else
layout(binding=0) writeonly buffer destBuffer{
    float data[];
} uOutput;
#endif
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location=2) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout(location=3) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
#ifdef WIDTH
#define LOGGP_AXES uInputSize.x
#define DPOS(spos,idx) ivec3(idx,spos.yz)
#endif
#ifdef HEIGHT
#define LOGGP_AXES uInputSize.y
#define DPOS(spos,idx) ivec3(spos.x,idx,spos.z)
#endif
#ifdef CHANNAL
#define LOGGP_AXES uInputSize.z
#define DPOS(spos,idx) ivec3(spos.xy,idx)
#endif
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    #ifdef NO_KEEPDIMS
    if (all(lessThan(pos.xy, uOutputSize.xy))) {
    #endif
    vec4 MaxData = texelFetch(uInput,DPOS(pos,0),0);
    vec4 MaxIdx = vec4(0);
    for (int i = 1; i < LOGGP_AXES; i++)
    {
        vec4 data = texelFetch(uInput,DPOS(pos,i),0);
        vec4 ChangeFlag = step(MaxData,data);
        MaxData = mix(MaxData,data,ChangeFlag);
        MaxIdx = mix(MaxIdx,vec4(i),ChangeFlag);
    }
    #ifdef CHANNAL
    MaxIdx = MaxIdx*vec4(4)+vec4(0,1,2,3);
    float tmpIndex = mix( MaxIdx[0], MaxIdx[1],  step( MaxData[0] , MaxData[1]));
    float tmpMax = max( MaxData[0] , MaxData[1] );
    tmpIndex = mix( tmpIndex ,MaxIdx[2],   step( tmpMax , MaxData[2]));
    tmpMax = max( tmpMax  , MaxData[2] );
    tmpIndex = mix( tmpIndex ,MaxIdx[3],   step( tmpMax , MaxData[3]));
    tmpMax = max( tmpMax  , MaxData[3] );
    MaxData[0] = tmpMax;
    MaxIdx[0] = tmpIndex;
    #endif
    #ifndef NO_KEEPDIMS
    imageStore(uOutput, pos, MaxIdx);
    #else
    int z = pos.z * 4;
    if(z+3 < uOutputSize.z){
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 0) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.r;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 1) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.g;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 2) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.b;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 3) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.a;
    }else if(z+2 < uOutputSize.z){
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 0) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.r;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 1) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.g;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 2) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.b;
    }else if(z+1 < uOutputSize.z){
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 0) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.r;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 1) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.g;
    }else if(z < uOutputSize.z){
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 0) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y ] = MaxIdx.r;
    }
    #endif
    #ifdef NO_KEEPDIMS
    }
    #endif
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(location=0) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(FORMAT, binding=1, location=1) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) readonly buffer sinput0{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
layout(binding = 1) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
#endif
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec2 uKernel;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uStride;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uPad;
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 11) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    ivec3 outputSize = uOutputSize;
    ivec2 spos = pos.xy * uStride - uPad;
#ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (all(lessThan(pos, outputSize)))
#endif
    {
        ivec2 inputSizeXY = uInputSize.xy;
        vec4 color = vec4(0.0);
        vec4 num = vec4(0.0);
        ivec2 sfxy = max(ivec2(0), -spos);
        ivec2 efxy = min(uKernel, inputSizeXY - spos);
        for (int fy = sfxy.y; fy < efxy.y; ++fy)
        {
            for (int fx = sfxy.x; fx < efxy.x; ++fx)
            {
                ivec3 spos_ = ivec3(spos + ivec2(fx, fy), pos.z);
                #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                color += texelFetch(uInput, spos_, 0);
                #else
                color += uInput.data[spos_.x + spos_.y * uInputSize.x + spos_.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
                #endif
                num += vec4(1.0);
            }
        }
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, color/num);
        #else
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = color / num;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput0;
layout(location=2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput1;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput0{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput0;
layout(binding = 2) readonly buffer sinput1{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput1;
#endif
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec2 uOutputTile;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec4 uWeightPosSwitch;
#ifdef RELU
layout(location = 7) uniform float uReluScale;
#endif
#ifdef PRELU
layout(location = 8) uniform vec4 uSlope[SLOPE_NUM];
#endif
#define COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos, w, h, tileW) ivec4(pos[0]%w, pos[1]%h, pos[2], pos[0]/w + (pos[1]/h)*tileW)
#define COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, w, h, tileW) ivec3(pos4[0] + (pos4[3]%tileW)*w, pos4[1] + (pos4[3]/tileW)*h, pos4[2])
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
#ifndef NO_BOUND
    if(all(lessThan(pos, ivec3(uOutputSize.xy * uOutputTile, uOutputSize.z))))
#endif
    {
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
    #ifdef MULTICAST
        ivec4 pos4 = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos, uOutputSize.x, uOutputSize.y, uOutputTile.x);
        pos4 = pos4 * uWeightPosSwitch;
        ivec3 wpos = COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, uOutputSize.x, uOutputSize.y, uOutputTile.x);
        vec4 weight = texelFetch(uInput1, wpos, 0);
        #ifdef ChannelBroadcast
            weight = vec4(weight.x);
        #endif
    #else
        vec4 weight = texelFetch(uInput1, pos, 0);
    #endif
    #ifdef ADD
        vec4 sum = texelFetch(uInput0, pos, 0) + weight;
    #endif
    #ifdef MUL
        vec4 sum = texelFetch(uInput0, pos, 0) * weight;
    #endif
    #ifdef SUB
        vec4 sum = texelFetch(uInput0, pos, 0) - weight;
    #endif
    #ifdef REALDIV
        vec4 sum = texelFetch(uInput0, pos, 0) / weight;
    #endif
    #ifdef POW
        vec4 sum = pow(texelFetch(uInput0, pos, 0), weight);
    #endif
    #ifdef RELU
        sum *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, sum));
    #endif
    #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uSlope[pos.z];
        sum *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, sum));
    #endif
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, sum);
#endif
    }
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location = 2) uniform ivec4 uInputShape;
layout(location = 3) uniform int AGroupCnum;
layout (local_size_x = 1, local_size_y = 1, local_size_z = 1) in;
vec4 getSumOfChannalValue(int posz) {
    vec4 sum = vec4(0);
    for (int h = 0; h < uInputShape.y; h++)
        for (int w = 0; w < uInputShape.x; w++) {
            sum += texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(w, h, posz), 0);
        }
    return sum;
vec4 calcAgeOf4Group(ivec4 group_idx) {
    ivec4 group_range_l = group_idx * ivec4(AGroupCnum);
    ivec4 group_range_h = group_range_l + ivec4(AGroupCnum - 1);
    vec4 each_group_sum = vec4(0);
    for (int oc4 = group_range_l.x / 4; oc4 < (group_range_h.w + 3) / 4; oc4++) {
        vec4 channal_sum = getSumOfChannalValue(oc4);
        int c = oc4 * 4;
        ivec4 c4 = ivec4(c, c + 1, c + 2, c + 3);
        
        vec4 ret =
            step(vec4(group_range_l), vec4(c4.x)) * step(vec4(c4.x), vec4(group_range_h));
        each_group_sum += vec4(channal_sum.x) * ret;
        
        ret =
            step(vec4(group_range_l), vec4(c4.y)) * step(vec4(c4.y), vec4(group_range_h));
        each_group_sum += vec4(channal_sum.y) * ret;
        
        ret =
            step(vec4(group_range_l), vec4(c4.z)) * step(vec4(c4.z), vec4(group_range_h));
        each_group_sum += vec4(channal_sum.z) * ret;
        
        ret =
            step(vec4(group_range_l), vec4(c4.w)) * step(vec4(c4.w), vec4(group_range_h));
        each_group_sum += vec4(channal_sum.w) * ret;
    }
    return each_group_sum / vec4(AGroupCnum * uInputShape.x * uInputShape.y);
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    ivec4 group_idx = (ivec4(pos.x * 4) + ivec4(0, 1, 2, 3)) / ivec4(AGroupCnum);
    vec4 group_age = calcAgeOf4Group(group_idx);
    uOutput.data[pos.x] = group_age;
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(binding = 2) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uAge;
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec4 uInputShape;
layout(location = 4) uniform int AGroupCnum;
layout(location = 5) uniform float EPS;
layout (local_size_x = 1, local_size_y = 1, local_size_z = 1) in;
vec4 getSumOfValueSubAge(int oc4, vec4 input_age)
    vec4 sum    = vec4(0);
    for(int h = 0; h < uInputShape.y; h++)
        for(int w = 0; w < uInputShape.x; w++)
        {
            vec4 input_value = texelFetch(uInput,ivec3(w,h,oc4),0);
            sum += pow(input_value-input_age,vec4(2));
        }
    return sum;
vec4 calcVarianceOf4Group(ivec4 group_idx)
    ivec4 group_range_l = group_idx * ivec4(AGroupCnum);
    ivec4 group_range_h = group_range_l + ivec4(AGroupCnum-1);
    vec4 each_group_variance = vec4(0);
    for(int oc4 = group_range_l.x / 4; oc4 < (group_range_h.w + 3) / 4; oc4++)
    {
        vec4 input_age = uAge.data[oc4];
        vec4 channal_sum = getSumOfValueSubAge(oc4,input_age);
        int c = oc4*4;
        ivec4 c4=ivec4(c,c+1,c+2,c+3);
        
        vec4 ret     = step(vec4(c4.x),vec4(group_range_h))*step(vec4(group_range_l),vec4(c4.x));
        each_group_variance += vec4(channal_sum.x)*ret;
        
        ret          = step(vec4(group_range_l),vec4(c4.y))*step(vec4(c4.y),vec4(group_range_h));
        each_group_variance += vec4(channal_sum.y)*ret;
        
        ret          = step(vec4(group_range_l),vec4(c4.z))*step(vec4(c4.z),vec4(group_range_h));
        each_group_variance += vec4(channal_sum.z)*ret;
        
        ret          = step(vec4(group_range_l),vec4(c4.w))*step(vec4(c4.w),vec4(group_range_h));
        each_group_variance += vec4(channal_sum.w)*ret;
    }
    return sqrt(each_group_variance/vec4(AGroupCnum*uInputShape.x*uInputShape.y)+vec4(EPS));
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    ivec4 group_idx = (ivec4(pos.x * 4) + ivec4(0, 1, 2, 3)) / ivec4(AGroupCnum);
    vec4 group_variance = calcVarianceOf4Group(group_idx);
    uOutput.data[pos.x] = group_variance;

        dataIn = max(dataIn, vec4(uClip.x));
        dataIn = min(dataIn, vec4(uClip.y));
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, dataIn);
        #else

        dataIn = max(dataIn, vec4(uClip.x));
        dataIn = min(dataIn, vec4(uClip.y));
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = dataIn;
        #endif
    }
#define COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos, w, h, tileW) ivec4(pos[0]%w, pos[1]%h, pos[2], pos[0]/w + (pos[1]/h)*tileW)
#define COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, w, h, tileW) ivec3(pos4[0] + (pos4[3]%tileW)*w, pos4[1] + (pos4[3]/tileW)*h, pos4[2])

#define c_exp_hi 88.3762626647949
#define c_exp_lo -88.3762626647949
vec4 exp_clamp(vec4 data) {
#ifdef MEDIUMP
    data = clamp(data, -11.089, 11.089);
#else
    data = clamp(data, c_exp_lo, c_exp_hi);
#endif
    return data;
vec4 exp_safe(vec4 data) {
    return exp(exp_clamp(data));
float exp_clamp1(float data) {
#ifdef MEDIUMP
    data = clamp(data, -11.089, 11.089);
#else
    data = clamp(data, c_exp_lo, c_exp_hi);
#endif
    return data;
float exp_safe1(float data) {
    return exp(exp_clamp1(data));

layout(location = 6) uniform int uOutputOffset;
layout(location = 7) uniform int ic;
layout(location = 8) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;

    vec4 data[];

void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (all(lessThan(pos, uBlitSize)))
    {
        ivec3 dstP = uDestOffset + pos;
        ivec3 srcP = uSourceOffset + pos;
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 in0 = texelFetch(uInput, srcP,0);
        #else
        vec4 in0 = uInput.data[ srcP.y*uBlitSize.x + srcP.x  + srcP.z*uBlitSize.x*uBlitSize.y ];
        #endif
        vec4 outColor = in0;
        if(uOutputOffset > 0) {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 in1 = texelFetch(uInput, srcP + ivec3(0, 0, 1),0);
            #else
            vec4 in1 = uInput.data[ srcP.y*uBlitSize.x + srcP.x  + (srcP.z)*uBlitSize.x*uBlitSize.y ];
            #endif
            if(uOutputOffset == 1){
                outColor.xyz    = in0.yzw;
                outColor.w      = in1.x;
            } else if(uOutputOffset == 2){
                outColor.xy     = in0.zw;
                outColor.zw     = in1.xy;
            } else {
                outColor.x      = in0.w;
                outColor.yzw    = in1.xyz;
            }
        }
        int mask = (ic - uOutputOffset) - pos.z * 4;
        if(4 - mask > 0) {
            ivec3 srcP1 = ivec3(srcP.xy, 0);
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 in1 = texelFetch(uInput2, srcP1,0);
            #else
            vec4 in1 = uInput2.data[srcP1.y*uInputSize2.x + srcP1.x  + srcP1.z*uInputSize2.x*uInputSize2.y];
            #endif
            if(mask == 1){
                outColor.yzw    = in1.xyz;
            } else if(mask == 2){
                outColor.zw     = in1.xy;
            } else if(mask == 3){
                outColor.w      = in1.x;
            } else {
                outColor = in1;
            }
        }
#ifdef BIAS
        int idx = uDestOffset.z + pos.z;
        outColor = outColor * uScale.data[idx] + uBias.data[idx];
        outColor *= mix(uSlope.data[idx], vec4(1.0), step(vec4(0.0), outColor));
#endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, dstP, outColor);
        #else
        uOutput.data[ dstP.y*uOutputSize.x + dstP.x  + dstP.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y ] = outColor;
        #endif
    }

    if (all(lessThan(pos, ivec3(uInputSize.xy * uInputTile.xy, uInputSize.z))))
    {
        vec4 outColor = texelFetch(uInput, pos,0);
        
        ivec4 pos4 = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputTile.x);
        pos4 += uOutputOffset;
        ivec3 dstP = COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, uOutputSize.x, uOutputSize.y, uOutputTile.x);

        int idx = pos.z;
        outColor = outColor * uScale.data[idx] + uBias.data[idx];
        outColor *= mix(uSlope.data[idx], vec4(1.0), step(vec4(0.0), outColor));
#endif
        imageStore(uOutput, dstP, outColor);
    }

    vec4 uSlope[SLOPE_NUM];

mat4 getW(int ic4, int oc4, int kernelx) {
    mat4 k = mat4(texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 0, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 1, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 2, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 3, oc4, kernelx), 0));
    return k;
#endif
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
#ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (all(lessThan(ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID), uOutputSize)))
#endif
    {
        ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
        int kernelX = uKernelSize.x;
        ivec3 inputSize = uInputSize;
        ivec2 s0 = pos.xy * uStride - uPad;
        int fx, fy, fz;
        ivec2 sfxy = max(ivec2(0), (UP_DIV(-s0, uDilate)));
        ivec2 efxy = min(uKernelSize, UP_DIV(inputSize.xy - s0, uDilate));
        vec4 color = vec4(0);
#ifdef BIAS
        #ifdef USE_UBO
        color = uBias[pos.z];
        #else
        color = uBias.data[pos.z];
        #endif
#endif
        int kernelY = pos.z;
        int groupIdx = pos.z / uOc4Group;
        for (fy = sfxy.y; fy < efxy.y; ++fy)
        {
            int sy = fy * uDilate.y + s0.y;
            for (fx = 0; fx < kernelX; ++fx)
            {
                int kernelZ = fx + fy * kernelX;
                int sx1 = fx * uDilate.x + s0.x;
                for (fz = 0; fz < inputSize.z; ++fz)
                {
                    int ic = fz + inputSize.z * groupIdx;
                    #ifdef USE_UBO
                    mat4 k = uKernel[(kernelY * inputSize.z + fz) * uFilterSize + kernelZ];
                    #else
                    int k_x = 4 * fz;
                    mat4 k = getW(k_x, kernelY, kernelZ);
                    #endif
                    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                    color += k * texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(sx1, sy, ic), 0);
                    #else
                    color += k * uInput.data[(inputSize.x*sy + sx1 + ic*inputSize.x*inputSize.y)];
                    #endif
                }
            }
        }
        #ifdef RELU
        color *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uSlope[pos.z];
        color *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef CLIP
        color = clamp(color, vec4(uClipMin), vec4(uClipMax));
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z), color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x * pos.y + pos.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = color;
        #endif
    }

mat4 getW(int ic4, int oc4, int kernelx) {
    mat4 k = mat4(texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 0, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 1, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 2, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 3, oc4, kernelx), 0));
    return k;
#endif

layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 outputSize = uOutputSize;
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (all(lessThan(ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID), outputSize)))
    #endif
    {
        ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID)*ivec3(1, 1, 1);
        ivec3 inputSize = uInputSize;
        int sy = pos.y;
        int sx = pos.x;
        int fx, fy, fz;
        vec4 colors[4];
        vec4 color = vec4(0);
#ifdef BIAS
        #ifdef USE_UBO
        color  = uBias[pos.z];
        #else
        color  = uBias.data[pos.z];
        #endif
#endif
        int kernelY = pos.z;
        int groupIdx = pos.z / uOc4Group;
        for (fz=0; fz<inputSize.z; ++fz)
        {
            int ic = fz + inputSize.z * groupIdx;
            #ifdef USE_UBO
            mat4 k = uKernel[kernelY * inputSize.z + fz];
            #else
            int kernelX = 4 * fz;
            mat4 k = getW(kernelX, kernelY, 0);
            #endif
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            color  += k*texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(sx+0, sy, ic),0);
            #else
            if( sy>=0 && sx +0 >= 0 ){
                color  += k * uInput.data[ (inputSize.x*sy + sx+0 + ic*inputSize.x*inputSize.y ) ];
            }
            #endif
        }
        #ifdef RELU
        color  *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uSlope[pos.z];
        color  *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef CLIP
        color = clamp(color, vec4(uClipMin), vec4(uClipMax));
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(pos.x + 0, pos.y, pos.z), color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x * pos.y + pos.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = color;
        #endif
    }

layout(location=7) uniform ivec2 uDilate;
layout(location = 9) uniform float uReluScale;
layout(location=10) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location=11) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout(location = 13) uniform vec4 uSlope[SLOPE_NUM];
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (all(lessThan(pos, uOutputSize)))
    #endif
    {
        ivec3 inputSize = uInputSize;
        ivec2 s0 = pos.xy * uStride - uPad;
        vec4 color = vec4(0);
        for (int fy = 0; fy < uKernelSize.y; fy++) {
            int sy = fy * uDilate.y + s0.y;
            for (int fx = 0; fx < uKernelSize.x; fx++) {
                int sx1 = fx * uDilate.x + s0.x;
                #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                vec4 k  = texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(fx, fy, pos.z), 0);
                color  += k * texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(sx1, sy, pos.z) ,0 ) ;
                #else
                vec4 k  = vec4( uKernel.data[fx + fy * uKernelSize.x + ((pos.z * 4) + 0) * uKernelSize.x * uKernelSize.y],
                                uKernel.data[fx + fy * uKernelSize.x + ((pos.z * 4) + 1) * uKernelSize.x * uKernelSize.y],
                                uKernel.data[fx + fy * uKernelSize.x + ((pos.z * 4) + 2) * uKernelSize.x * uKernelSize.y],
                                uKernel.data[fx + fy * uKernelSize.x + ((pos.z * 4) + 3) * uKernelSize.x * uKernelSize.y]);
                if( sx1 >= 0 && sy>=0 ){
                    color  += k * uInput.data[sx1 + sy * uInputSize.x + pos.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y] ;
                }
                #endif
            }
        }
        #ifdef HAS_BIAS
        color += uBias.data[pos.z];
        #endif
        #ifdef RELU
        color *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uSlope[pos.z];
        color   *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = color;
        #endif
    }
