layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location = 2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uKernel;
layout(binding = 3) readonly buffer bias{
    vec4 data[];
} uBias;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec2 uPad;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uKernelSize;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uStride;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uDilate;
#ifdef RELU
layout(location = 9) uniform float uReluScale;
#endif
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 11) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout(location = 12) uniform int uGroupChannel;
layout(location = 13) uniform int uOcGroup;
#define UP_DIV(x, y) (((x) + (y) - 1)/(y))
#ifdef PRELU
layout(location = 14) uniform vec4 uSlope[SLOPE_NUM];
#endif
#ifdef CLIP
layout(location = 30) uniform float uClipMin;
layout(location = 31) uniform float uClipMax;
#endif
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    if (all(lessThan(ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID), uOutputSize)))
    {
        ivec3 pos       = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
        ivec2 s0        = pos.xy * uStride - uPad;
        int fx, fy, fz, i;
        ivec2 sfxy  = max(ivec2(0), (UP_DIV(-s0, uDilate)));
        ivec2 efxy  = min(uKernelSize, UP_DIV(uInputSize.xy - s0, uDilate));
        ivec4 oc = ivec4(pos.z * 4) + ivec4(0,1,2,3);
        ivec4 group_ic_begin = oc / ivec4(uOcGroup) * ivec4(uGroupChannel);
        vec4 color  = uBias.data[pos.z];
        for (fy = sfxy.y; fy < efxy.y; ++fy)
        {
            int sy = fy * uDilate.y + s0.y;
            for (fx = sfxy.x; fx < efxy.x; ++fx)
            {
                int sx = fx * uDilate.x + s0.x;
                for(i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
                {
                    for (fz = 0; fz < uGroupChannel; ++fz)
                    {
                        int ic = group_ic_begin[i] + fz;
                        float k = texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(fz, pos.z, fx + fy * uKernelSize.x), 0)[i];
                        float x = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(sx, sy, ic/4),0)[ic%4];
                        color[i]  += k * x;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        #ifdef RELU
        color  *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uSlope[pos.z];
        color  *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef CLIP
        color = clamp(color, vec4(uClipMin), vec4(uClipMax));
        #endif
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, color);
    }
layout(std430) buffer;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0)  writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
#ifndef USE_UBO
layout(location = 2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uKernel;
layout(binding = 3) readonly buffer bias{
    vec4 data[];
} uBias;
#endif
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec2 uPad;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uKernelSize;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uStride;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uDilate;
#ifdef RELU
layout(location = 9) uniform float uReluScale;
#endif
#ifdef CLIP
layout(location = 30) uniform float uClipMin;
layout(location = 31) uniform float uClipMax;
#endif
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 11) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout(location = 12) uniform int uOc4Group;
#define UP_DIV(x, y) (((x) + (y) - 1)/(y))
#ifdef USE_UBO
layout(location = 14) uniform int uFilterSize;
layout(std140) uniform Weights {
    vec4 uBias[SLOPE_NUM];
    vec4 uSlope[SLOPE_NUM];
    mat4 uKernel[K_SIZE];
#else
layout(location = 14) uniform vec4 uSlope[SLOPE_NUM];
mat4 getW(int ic4, int oc4, int kernelx) {
    mat4 k = mat4(texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 0, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 1, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 2, oc4, kernelx), 0),
            texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(ic4 + 3, oc4, kernelx), 0));
    return k;
#endif
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (all(lessThan(ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID), uOutputSize)))
    #endif
    {
        ivec3 pos       = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
        int kernelX     = 3;
        ivec3 inputSize = uInputSize;
        ivec2 s0        = pos.xy - 1;
        int fx, fy, fz;
        ivec2 sfxy  = max(ivec2(0), -s0);
        ivec2 efxy  = min(ivec2(3), inputSize.xy - s0);
        vec4 color = vec4(0);
#ifdef BIAS
        #ifdef USE_UBO
        color  = uBias[pos.z];
        #else
        color  = uBias.data[pos.z];
        #endif
#endif
        int kernelY = pos.z;
        int groupIdx = pos.z / uOc4Group;
        for (fy = sfxy.y; fy < efxy.y; ++fy)
        {
            int sy = fy + s0.y;
            for (fx = 0; fx < kernelX; ++fx)
            {
                int kernelZ = fx + fy * kernelX;
                int sx1 = fx + s0.x;
                for (fz = 0; fz < inputSize.z; ++fz)
                {
                    int ic = fz + inputSize.z * groupIdx;
                    #ifdef USE_UBO
                    mat4 k = uKernel[(kernelY * inputSize.z + fz) * uFilterSize + kernelZ];
                    #else
                    int k_x = 4 * fz;
                    mat4 k = getW(k_x, kernelY, kernelZ);
                    #endif
                    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                    color  += k * texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(sx1, sy, ic), 0);
                    #else
                    color  += k * uInput.data[(inputSize.x * sy + sx1 + ic * inputSize.x * inputSize.y)];
                    #endif
                }
            }
        }
        #ifdef RELU
        color  *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uSlope[pos.z];
        color  *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef CLIP
        color = clamp(color, vec4(uClipMin), vec4(uClipMax));
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z), color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x * pos.y + pos.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = color;
        #endif
    }
layout(std430) buffer;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(location = 2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uKernel;
layout(binding = 3) readonly buffer bias{
    vec4 data[];
} uBias;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec2 uPad;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uKernelSize;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uStride;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uDilate;
layout(location = 8) uniform float uReluScale;
layout(location = 9) uniform int uReluOffset;
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 11) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
#define IN_BOUNDS(p, p0, p1) (all(greaterThanEqual(p, p0)) && all(lessThan(p, p1)))
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos   = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (all(lessThan(pos, uOutputSize)))
    {
        ivec2 p0  = ivec2(mod( mod( vec2(uPad + uStride - pos.xy) , vec2(uStride)) , vec2(uStride)));
        vec4 color  = uBias.data[pos.z];
        for (int y = p0.y; y < uKernelSize.y; y += uStride.y) {
            for (int x = p0.x; x < uKernelSize.x; x += uStride.x) {
                int i = y * uKernelSize.x + x ;
                ivec2 idx = pos.xy + ivec2(x, y) - uPad;
                if (IN_BOUNDS(idx, ivec2(0), ivec2(uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y) * uStride)) {
                    ivec2 coord = idx / uStride;
                    for (int l = 0; l < uInputSize.z; ++l) {
                        int kernelX = 4 * l;
                        vec4 k0 = texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(kernelX + 0, pos.z, i), 0);
                        vec4 k1 = texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(kernelX + 1, pos.z, i), 0);
                        vec4 k2 = texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(kernelX + 2, pos.z, i), 0);
                        vec4 k3 = texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(kernelX + 3, pos.z, i), 0);
                        mat4 k = mat4(k0, k1, k2, k3);
                        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                        color += k * texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(coord.x, coord.y, l), 0);
                        #else
                        color   += k * uInput.data[ (uInputSize.x*coord.y + coord.x  + l*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y ) ] ;
                        #endif
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        #ifdef RELU
        color   *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uBias.data[pos.z + uReluOffset];
        color   *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z), color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x * pos.y + pos.x +  pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = color;
        #endif
    }
layout(std430) buffer;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location = 2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uKernel;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
}uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
}uInput;
layout(binding = 2) readonly buffer skernel{
    float data[];
}uKernel;
#endif
layout(binding = 3) readonly buffer bias{
    vec4 data[];
} uBias;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec2 uPad;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec3 uKernelSize;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uStride;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uDilate;
layout(location = 8) uniform float uReluScale;
layout(location = 9) uniform int uReluOffset;
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 11) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
#define IN_BOUNDS(p, p0, p1) (all(greaterThanEqual(p, p0)) && all(lessThan(p, p1)))
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos   = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (all(lessThan(pos, uOutputSize)))
    {
        ivec2 p0  = ivec2(mod( mod( vec2(uPad + uStride - pos.xy) , vec2(uStride)) , vec2(uStride)));
        vec4 color  = uBias.data[pos.z];
        for (int y = p0.y; y < uKernelSize.y; y += uStride.y) {
            for (int x = p0.x; x < uKernelSize.x; x += uStride.x) {
                int i = y * uKernelSize.x + x;
                ivec2 idx = pos.xy + ivec2(x, y) - uPad;
                if (IN_BOUNDS(idx, ivec2(0), ivec2(uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y) * uStride)) {
                    ivec2 coord = idx / uStride;
                    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                    vec4 k = texelFetch(uKernel, ivec3(uKernelSize.x - 1 - x, uKernelSize.y - 1 - y, pos.z), 0);
                    color += k * texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(coord.x, coord.y, pos.z), 0);
                    #else
                    int fx = uKernelSize.x - 1 - x;
                    int fy = uKernelSize.y - 1 - y;
                    int p0 = fx + fy * uKernelSize.x + ((pos.z * 4) + 0) * uKernelSize.x * uKernelSize.y;
                    int p1 = fx + fy * uKernelSize.x + ((pos.z * 4) + 1) * uKernelSize.x * uKernelSize.y;
                    int p2 = fx + fy * uKernelSize.x + ((pos.z * 4) + 2) * uKernelSize.x * uKernelSize.y;
                    int p3 = fx + fy * uKernelSize.x + ((pos.z * 4) + 3) * uKernelSize.x * uKernelSize.y;
                    vec4 k  = vec4(uKernel.data[p0],
                                    uKernel.data[p1],
                                    uKernel.data[p2],
                                    uKernel.data[p3]);
                    int inpos = coord.x + coord.y * uInputSize.x + pos.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y;
                    color  += k * uInput.data[inpos];
                    #endif
                }
            }
        }
        #ifdef RELU
        color   *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uBias.data[pos.z + uReluOffset];
        color   *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color));
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z), color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = color;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
#endif
layout(location = 15) uniform float uReluScale;
layout(location = 16) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 17) uniform int uUnroll;
layout(location = 18) uniform vec4 uRelu[SLOPE_NUM];
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (all(lessThan(pos, uOutputSize)))
    {
        ivec3 posUnrool = pos * ivec3(uUnroll, 1, 1);
        vec4 color0 = MAINOP(posUnrool + ivec3(0, 0, 0));
        vec4 color1 = MAINOP(posUnrool + ivec3(1, 0, 0));
        vec4 color2 = MAINOP(posUnrool + ivec3(2, 0, 0));
        vec4 color3 = MAINOP(posUnrool + ivec3(3, 0, 0));
        #ifdef RELU
        color0 *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color0));
        color1 *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color1));
        color2 *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color2));
        color3 *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, color3));
        #endif
        #ifdef PRELU
        vec4 reluSlop = uRelu[posUnrool.z];
        color0 *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color0));
        color1 *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color1));
        color2 *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color2));
        color3 *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, color3));
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, posUnrool + ivec3(0, 0, 0), color0);
        imageStore(uOutput, posUnrool + ivec3(1, 0, 0), color1);
        imageStore(uOutput, posUnrool + ivec3(2, 0, 0), color2);
        imageStore(uOutput, posUnrool + ivec3(3, 0, 0), color3);
        #else
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x*posUnrool.y + posUnrool.x + 0 + posUnrool.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y] = color0;
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x*posUnrool.y + posUnrool.x + 1 + posUnrool.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y] = color1;
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x*posUnrool.y + posUnrool.x + 2 + posUnrool.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y] = color2;
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x*posUnrool.y + posUnrool.x + 3 + posUnrool.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y] = color3;
        #endif
    }
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location = 2) uniform float alpha_;
layout(local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
#define c_exp_hi 88.3762626647949
#define c_exp_lo -88.3762626647949
vec4 exp_clamp(vec4 data) {
    #ifdef MEDIUMP
    data = clamp(data, -11.089, 11.089);
    #else
        data = clamp(data, c_exp_lo, c_exp_hi);
    #endif
    return data;
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    vec4 data = texelFetch(uInput, pos, 0);
    vec4 flag = step(vec4(0), data);
    vec4 elu_data = (vec4(alpha_) * (exp(exp_clamp(data)) - vec4(1))) * (vec4(1) - flag) +
                    data * flag;
    imageStore(uOutput, pos, elu_data);
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uImage;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
#endif
uniform highp sampler2D uInput2D;
#ifdef HAS_MASK
uniform highp sampler2D uMaskTex;
#endif
uniform highp vec4 uMean;
uniform highp vec4 uNorm;
uniform highp vec2 uUvOffset;
uniform highp vec2 uUvDx;
uniform highp vec2 uUvDy;
uniform int uWidth;
uniform int uHeight;
layout (local_size_x = 8, local_size_y = 8, local_size_z = 1) in;
void main()
    ivec2 pos = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (pos.x >= uWidth || pos.y >= uHeight) return;
    highp float tx = (float(pos.x) + 0.5) / float(uWidth);
    highp float ty = (float(pos.y) + 0.5) / float(uHeight);
    highp vec2 uv = uUvOffset + tx * uUvDx + ty * uUvDy;
    highp vec4 color = texture(uInput2D, uv);
#ifdef HAS_MASK
    color.w = texture(uMaskTex, uv).y;
#endif
#ifdef CHANNEL_1
    color = vec4(color.r, color.r, color.r, 0.0);
#elif defined(CHANNEL_2)
    color = vec4(color.g, color.g, color.g, color.g);
#endif
    color = (color - uMean) * uNorm;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
    imageStore(uImage, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, 0), color);
#else
    uOutput.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x] = color;
#endif
layout(std430) buffer;
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;    
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uV;                       
layout(location = 2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uWeight;                  
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec2 uRoundSize;   
layout(location = 11) uniform int uIc4;            
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    int tile_w    = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.x);
    int tile_h    = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.y);
    int z_out     = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.z);   
    ivec2 rs = uRoundSize;
    int oc4_idx   = z_out / 16;
    int alpha_idx = z_out - oc4_idx * 16;
    if (tile_w >= rs.x || tile_h >= rs.y || alpha_idx >= 16)
        return;
    vec4 acc = vec4(0.0);
    for (int ic4 = 0; ic4 < uIc4; ++ic4) {
        
        vec4 v = texelFetch(uV, ivec3(tile_w, tile_h, ic4 * 16 + alpha_idx), 0);
        
        int base_x = ic4 * 4;
        vec4 u0 = texelFetch(uWeight, ivec3(base_x,     oc4_idx, alpha_idx), 0);
        vec4 u1 = texelFetch(uWeight, ivec3(base_x + 1, oc4_idx, alpha_idx), 0);
        vec4 u2 = texelFetch(uWeight, ivec3(base_x + 2, oc4_idx, alpha_idx), 0);
        vec4 u3 = texelFetch(uWeight, ivec3(base_x + 3, oc4_idx, alpha_idx), 0);
        acc += v.x * u0 + v.y * u1 + v.z * u2 + v.w * u3;
    }
    imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(tile_w, tile_h, z_out), acc);
layout(std430) buffer;
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec2 uPad;
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;   
layout(location = 11) uniform ivec2 uRoundSize;   
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    int tile_w  = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.x);
    int tile_h  = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.y);
    int ic4_idx = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.z);
    ivec2 rs = uRoundSize;
    if (tile_w >= rs.x || tile_h >= rs.y || ic4_idx >= uInputSize.z)
        return;
    int xs = tile_w * 2 - uPad.x;
    int ys = tile_h * 2 - uPad.y;
    ivec3 inSz = uInputSize;
    vec4 d[4][4];
    for (int dy = 0; dy < 4; ++dy) {
        int sy = ys + dy;
        for (int dx = 0; dx < 4; ++dx) {
            int sx = xs + dx;
            d[dy][dx] = (sx >= 0 && sx < inSz.x && sy >= 0 && sy < inSz.y)
                        ? texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(sx, sy, ic4_idx), 0)
                        : vec4(0.0);
        }
    }
    vec4 t[4][4];
    for (int x = 0; x < 4; ++x) {
        t[0][x] =  d[0][x] - d[2][x];
        t[1][x] =  0.5 * d[1][x] + 0.5 * d[2][x];
        t[2][x] = -0.5 * d[1][x] + 0.5 * d[2][x];
        t[3][x] = -d[1][x] + d[3][x];
    }
    int z_base = ic4_idx * 16;
    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
        vec4 v0 =  t[r][0] - t[r][2];
        vec4 v1 =  0.5 * t[r][1] + 0.5 * t[r][2];
        vec4 v2 = -0.5 * t[r][1] + 0.5 * t[r][2];
        vec4 v3 = -t[r][1] + t[r][3];
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(tile_w, tile_h, z_base + r * 4 + 0), v0);
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(tile_w, tile_h, z_base + r * 4 + 1), v1);
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(tile_w, tile_h, z_base + r * 4 + 2), v2);
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(tile_w, tile_h, z_base + r * 4 + 3), v3);
    }
layout(std430) buffer;
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uM;
layout(binding = 3) readonly buffer bias {
    vec4 data[];
} uBias;
#ifdef RELU
layout(location = 9) uniform float uReluScale;
#endif
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;   
layout(location = 11) uniform ivec2 uRoundSize;     
layout(location = 12) uniform vec4 uSlope[SLOPE_NUM];
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    int tile_w  = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.x);
    int tile_h  = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.y);
    int oc4_idx = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.z);
    ivec2 rs = uRoundSize;
    ivec3 outSz = uOutputSize;
    if (tile_w >= rs.x || tile_h >= rs.y || oc4_idx >= outSz.z)
        return;
    int z_base = oc4_idx * 16;
    vec4 m[4][4];
    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
            m[r][c] = texelFetch(uM, ivec3(tile_w, tile_h, z_base + r * 4 + c), 0);
        }
    }
    vec4 s0[4], s1[4];
    for (int x = 0; x < 4; ++x) {
        s0[x] = m[x][0] + m[x][1] + m[x][2];
        s1[x] = m[x][1] - m[x][2] + m[x][3];
    }
    vec4 bias_val = vec4(0.0);
#ifdef BIAS
    bias_val = uBias.data[oc4_idx];
#endif
    vec4 res00 = bias_val + s0[0] + s0[1] + s0[2];   
    vec4 res10 = bias_val + s0[1] - s0[2] + s0[3];   
    vec4 res01 = bias_val + s1[0] + s1[1] + s1[2];   
    vec4 res11 = bias_val + s1[1] - s1[2] + s1[3];   
#ifdef RELU
    float rs_val = uReluScale;
    res00 *= mix(vec4(rs_val), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, res00));
    res10 *= mix(vec4(rs_val), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, res10));
    res01 *= mix(vec4(rs_val), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, res01));
    res11 *= mix(vec4(rs_val), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, res11));
#endif
#ifdef PRELU
    vec4 slp = uSlope[oc4_idx];
    res00 *= mix(slp, vec4(1.0), step(0.0, res00));
    res10 *= mix(slp, vec4(1.0), step(0.0, res10));
    res01 *= mix(slp, vec4(1.0), step(0.0, res01));
    res11 *= mix(slp, vec4(1.0), step(0.0, res11));
#endif
    int ow0 = tile_w * 2;
    int ow1 = ow0 + 1;
    int oh0 = tile_h * 2;
    int oh1 = oh0 + 1;
    if (ow0 < outSz.x && oh0 < outSz.y)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(ow0, oh0, oc4_idx), res00);
    if (ow1 < outSz.x && oh0 < outSz.y)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(ow1, oh0, oc4_idx), res10);
    if (ow0 < outSz.x && oh1 < outSz.y)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(ow0, oh1, oc4_idx), res01);
    if (ow1 < outSz.x && oh1 < outSz.y)
        imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(ow1, oh1, oc4_idx), res11);
layout(std430) buffer;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(binding=2) readonly buffer weight{
    vec4 data[];
} uKernel;
layout(binding=3) readonly buffer bias{
    vec4 data[];
} uBias;
uniform float uReluScale;
uniform int uDstDepth;
uniform int uSrcDepth;
uniform ivec2 uInputSize;
uniform ivec2 uOutputSize;
uniform vec4 uSlope[SLOPE_NUM];
uniform ivec2 uInTile;
uniform ivec2 uOutTile;
shared vec4 sh_mem[YLOCAL * XLOCAL];
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    const int threads = int(gl_WorkGroupSize.y);
    const int workers = int(gl_WorkGroupSize.x);
    ivec3 tid = ivec3(gl_LocalInvocationID);
    ivec3 gid = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    int batch_id = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.z);
    vec4 value_0 = vec4(0);
    if (gid.x < uDstDepth) {
        int offset = 4 * gid.x * uSrcDepth + 4 * tid.y;
        int iterations = (uSrcDepth + threads-1) / threads;
        for (int d = 0; d < iterations; d++, offset += 4 * threads) {
            int z_slice = d*threads+tid.y;
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            ivec3 in_tex = ivec3(
                (batch_id % uInTile.x) * uInputSize.x,
                (batch_id / uInTile.x) * uInputSize.y,
                z_slice);
            vec4 src = texelFetch(uInput, in_tex, 0);
            #else
            int plane = uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y;
            vec4 src = uInput.data[batch_id * uSrcDepth * plane + z_slice * plane];
            #endif
            vec4 uKd0 = uKernel.data[offset + 0];
            vec4 uKd1 = uKernel.data[offset + 1];
            vec4 uKd2 = uKernel.data[offset + 2];
            vec4 uKd3 = uKernel.data[offset + 3];
            value_0.x += dot(src, uKd0);
            value_0.y += dot(src, uKd1);
            value_0.z += dot(src, uKd2);
            value_0.w += dot(src, uKd3);
        }
        sh_mem[workers * tid.y + tid.x] = value_0;
    }
    memoryBarrierShared();
    barrier();
    if (tid.y > 0 || gid.x >= uDstDepth) {
        return;
    }
    for (int t = 1; t < threads; t++) {
        value_0 += sh_mem[workers * t + tid.x];
    }
    value_0 += uBias.data[gid.x];
#ifdef RELU
    value_0 *= mix(vec4(uReluScale), vec4(1.0), step(0.0, value_0));
#endif
#ifdef PRELU
    vec4 reluSlop = uSlope[gid.x];
    value_0   *= mix(reluSlop, vec4(1), step(0.0, value_0));
#endif
    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
    ivec3 out_tex = ivec3(
        (batch_id % uOutTile.x) * uOutputSize.x,
        (batch_id / uOutTile.x) * uOutputSize.y,
        gid.x);
    imageStore(uOutput, out_tex, value_0);
    #else
    int out_plane = uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y;
    uOutput.data[batch_id * uDstDepth * out_plane + gid.x * out_plane] = value_0;
    #endif
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(binding = 2) readonly buffer sindices{
    int data[];
} uIndices;
layout(location=3) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout(location=4) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uInputTile;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uOutputTile;
layout(location = 7) uniform int uIndicesCount;
#define COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos , w, h, tileW) ivec4(pos[0]%w, pos[1]%h, pos[2], pos[0]/w + (pos[1]/h)*tileW)
#define COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, w, h, tileW) ivec3(pos4[0] + (pos4[3]%tileW)*w, pos4[1] + (pos4[3]/tileW)*h, pos4[2])
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if(all(lessThan(pos, ivec3(uOutputSize.xy * uOutputTile, uOutputSize.z))))
    {
#if ( AXIS == 0 )
        ivec4 out_pos = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos,uOutputSize[0],uOutputSize[1],uOutputTile.x);
        ivec4 src_pos4 = ivec4(out_pos[0],out_pos[1],out_pos[2], uIndices.data[out_pos[3]]);
        ivec3 in_pos = COORDTRANS_4TO3(src_pos4,uInputSize[0],uInputSize[1],uInputTile.x);
        vec4 value = texelFetch(uInput, in_pos, 0);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, value);
#endif
#if ( AXIS == 1 )
        ivec4 out_pos = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos,uOutputSize[0],uOutputSize[1],uOutputTile.x);
        int z = out_pos[2];
        ivec4 Indices = ivec4(  uIndices.data[ 4*z + 0 ]  , uIndices.data[ 4*z + 1 ]  ,
                                uIndices.data[ 4*z + 2 ]  , uIndices.data[ 4*z + 3 ]  );
        ivec4 id =  Indices / 4 ;
        ivec4 id_r =  ivec4( mod( vec4(Indices) , 4.0 ));
        ivec4 src_pos_0 = ivec4(out_pos[0], out_pos[1], id[0], out_pos[3]);
        ivec4 src_pos_1 = ivec4(out_pos[0], out_pos[1], id[1], out_pos[3]);
        ivec4 src_pos_2 = ivec4(out_pos[0], out_pos[1], id[2], out_pos[3]);
        ivec4 src_pos_3 = ivec4(out_pos[0], out_pos[1], id[3], out_pos[3]);
        ivec3 in_pos_0 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(src_pos_0,uInputSize[0],uInputSize[1],uInputTile.x);
        ivec3 in_pos_1 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(src_pos_1,uInputSize[0],uInputSize[1],uInputTile.x);
        ivec3 in_pos_2 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(src_pos_2,uInputSize[0],uInputSize[1],uInputTile.x);
        ivec3 in_pos_3 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(src_pos_3,uInputSize[0],uInputSize[1],uInputTile.x);
        vec4 value_0 = texelFetch(uInput, in_pos_0, 0);
        vec4 value_1 = texelFetch(uInput, in_pos_1, 0);
        vec4 value_2 = texelFetch(uInput, in_pos_2, 0);
        vec4 value_3 = texelFetch(uInput, in_pos_3, 0);
        vec4 value = vec4( value_0[id_r[0]] , value_1[id_r[1]] ,value_2[id_r[2]],value_3[id_r[3]]);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, value);
#endif
#if ( AXIS == 2 )
        ivec4 out_pos = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos,uOutputSize[0],uOutputSize[1],uOutputTile.x);
        ivec4 src_pos4 = ivec4(out_pos[0],uIndices.data[out_pos[1]],out_pos[2], out_pos[3]);
        ivec3 in_pos = COORDTRANS_4TO3(src_pos4,uInputSize[0],uInputSize[1],uInputTile.x);
        vec4 value = texelFetch(uInput, in_pos, 0);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, value);
#endif
#if ( AXIS == 3 )
        ivec4 out_pos = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos,uOutputSize[0],uOutputSize[1],uOutputTile.x);
        ivec4 src_pos4 = ivec4(uIndices.data[out_pos[0]],out_pos[1],out_pos[2], out_pos[3]);
        ivec3 in_pos = COORDTRANS_4TO3(src_pos4,uInputSize[0],uInputSize[1],uInputTile.x);
        vec4 value = texelFetch(uInput, in_pos, 0);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, value);
#endif
    }
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(binding = 2) readonly buffer sinput1{
    vec4 data[];
} uAge;
layout(binding = 3) readonly buffer sinput2{
    vec4 data[];
} uVariance;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    vec4 data   = texelFetch(uInput, pos,0);
    vec4 vAge = uAge.data[pos.z];
    vec4 vVariance = uVariance.data[pos.z];
    vec4 color = (data-vAge)/vVariance;
    imageStore(uOutput,pos,color);
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location = 2) uniform vec4 alpha_;
layout(location = 3) uniform vec4 beta_;
layout(local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    vec4 data = clamp(alpha_*texelFetch(uInput, pos, 0)+beta_,vec4(0.0),vec4(1.0));
    imageStore(uOutput, pos, data);
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    vec4 in_data = texelFetch(uInput, pos, 0);
    vec4 out_data = in_data * max(vec4(0),min(vec4(1),in_data*0.1666667+0.5));
    imageStore(uOutput, pos, out_data);
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image2D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location = 2) uniform ivec3 uImgSize;
layout (local_size_x = 8, local_size_y = 8, local_size_z = 1) in;
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (all(lessThan(pos, uImgSize))) {
        vec4 dataIn = texelFetch(uInput, pos, 0);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos.xy, dataIn);
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) readonly uniform PRECISION image3D uImage;
#else
layout(binding = 0) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(binding=1) writeonly buffer destBuffer{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutBuffer;
layout(location = 2) uniform int uWidth;
layout(location = 3) uniform int uHeight;
layout (local_size_x = 8, local_size_y = 8, local_size_z = 1) in;
void main()
    ivec2 pos = ivec2(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (pos.x >= uWidth || pos.y >= uHeight) return;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
    vec4 color = imageLoad(uImage, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, 0));
#else
    vec4 color = uInput.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x];
#endif
    uOutBuffer.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x] = color;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(location=0) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uImage;
#else
layout(binding = 0) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(binding=1) writeonly buffer destBuffer{
    float data[];
} uOutBuffer;
layout(location = 2) uniform int uWidth;
layout(location = 3) uniform int uHeight;
layout(location = 4) uniform int uChannel;
layout(location = 5) uniform int uBatch;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uTile;
layout (local_size_x = 8, local_size_y = 8, local_size_z = 1) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
#ifdef MULTI_BATCH
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (pos.x < uTile.x * uWidth && pos.y < uTile.y * uHeight)
    #endif
    {
        int w = pos.x % uWidth;
        int h = pos.y % uHeight;
        int c = pos.z * 4;
        int b = (pos.y / uHeight) * uTile.x + (pos.x / uWidth);
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 color = texelFetch(uImage, pos, 0);
        #else
        vec4 color = uInput.data[uWidth * uBatch * pos.y + pos.x + pos.z * uWidth * uBatch * uHeight];
        #endif
        if ((c + 0) < uChannel) uOutBuffer.data[w + uWidth * h + (c + 0) * uWidth * uHeight + b * uWidth * uHeight * uChannel] = color.r;
        if ((c + 1) < uChannel) uOutBuffer.data[w + uWidth * h + (c + 1) * uWidth * uHeight + b * uWidth * uHeight * uChannel] = color.g;
        if ((c + 2) < uChannel) uOutBuffer.data[w + uWidth * h + (c + 2) * uWidth * uHeight + b * uWidth * uHeight * uChannel] = color.b;
        if ((c + 3) < uChannel) uOutBuffer.data[w + uWidth * h + (c + 3) * uWidth * uHeight + b * uWidth * uHeight * uChannel] = color.a;
    }
#else
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (pos.x < uWidth && pos.y < uHeight)
    #endif
    {
        int z = pos.z * 4;
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 color = texelFetch(uImage, pos, 0);
        #else
        vec4 color = uInput.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x + pos.z * uWidth * uHeight];
        #endif
        if ((z + 0) < uChannel) uOutBuffer.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x + (z + 0) * uWidth * uHeight] = color.r;
        if ((z + 1) < uChannel) uOutBuffer.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x + (z + 1) * uWidth * uHeight] = color.g;
        if ((z + 2) < uChannel) uOutBuffer.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x + (z + 2) * uWidth * uHeight] = color.b;
        if ((z + 3) < uChannel) uOutBuffer.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x + (z + 3) * uWidth * uHeight] = color.a;
    }
#endif
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput0;
layout(location=2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput1;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput0{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput0;
layout(binding = 2) readonly buffer sinput1{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput1;
#endif
layout(location=3) uniform ivec4 uInputSize0;
layout(location=4) uniform ivec4 uInputSize1;
layout(location=5) uniform ivec4 uOutputSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
vec4 PixelMul(int i, ivec3 pos)
    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 data0 = texelFetch(uInput0, ivec3(i, pos.y, pos.z), 0);
        #ifdef CHWxHW
            vec4 data1 = texelFetch(uInput1, ivec3(pos.x,i, 0), 0);
            data1 = vec4(data1.x); 
        #else
            vec4 data1 = texelFetch(uInput1, ivec3(pos.x, i, pos.z), 0);
        #endif
    #else
        vec4 data0 = uInput0.data[i + pos.y * uInputSize0.x + pos.z * uInputSize0.x * uInputSize0.y];
        #ifdef CHWxHW
            vec4 data1 = uInput1.data[i + pos.x * uInputSize1.y];
            data1 = vec4(data1.x); 
        #else
            vec4 data1 = uInput1.data[i + pos.x * uInputSize1.y + pos.z * uInputSize1.x * uInputSize1.y];
        #endif
    #endif
    return data0 * data1;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID) * ivec3(2, 2, 1);
    if(all(lessThan(pos, uOutputSize.xyz)))
    {
        vec4 outData00 = vec4(0);
        vec4 outData01 = vec4(0);
        vec4 outData10 = vec4(0);
        vec4 outData11 = vec4(0);
        for(int i = 0; i < uInputSize0.x; i++)
        {
            outData00 += PixelMul(i, pos + ivec3(0, 0, 0));
            outData01 += PixelMul(i, pos + ivec3(1, 0, 0));
            outData10 += PixelMul(i, pos + ivec3(0, 1, 0));
            outData11 += PixelMul(i, pos + ivec3(1, 1, 0));
        }
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos + ivec3(0, 0, 0), outData00);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos + ivec3(1, 0, 0), outData01);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos + ivec3(0, 1, 0), outData10);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos + ivec3(1, 1, 0), outData11);
        #else
        uOutput.data[pos.x + 0 + (pos.y + 0) * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = outData00;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + 1 + (pos.y + 0) * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = outData01;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + 0 + (pos.y + 1) * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = outData10;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + 1 + (pos.y + 1) * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = outData11;
        #endif
    }
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput0;
layout(location=2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput1;
layout(location=3) uniform ivec4 uInputSize0;   
layout(location=4) uniform ivec4 uInputSize1;   
layout(location=5) uniform ivec4 uOutputSize;   
#ifndef WG_X
#define WG_X  16
#endif
#ifndef WG_Y
#define WG_Y  16
#endif
#ifndef TILE_R
#define TILE_R 4
#endif
#ifndef TILE_C
#define TILE_C 4
#endif
#ifndef TILE_K
#define TILE_K 8
#endif
#define TS_M (WG_Y * TILE_R)   
#define TS_N (WG_X * TILE_C)   
layout (local_size_x = WG_X, local_size_y = WG_Y, local_size_z = 1) in;
shared vec4 tileA[TS_M * TILE_K];  
shared vec4 tileB[TILE_K * TS_N];  
void main()
    int lx = int(gl_LocalInvocationID.x);   
    int ly = int(gl_LocalInvocationID.y);   
    int gz = int(gl_GlobalInvocationID.z);   
    int wg_col = int(gl_WorkGroupID.x) * TS_N;   
    int wg_row = int(gl_WorkGroupID.y) * TS_M;   
    int K = uInputSize0.x;   
    int oH = uOutputSize.y;
    int oW = uOutputSize.x;
#ifdef CHWxHW
    int zA = gz;
    int zB = 0;  
#else
    int zA = gz;
    int zB = gz;
#endif
    vec4 acc[TILE_R][TILE_C];
    for (int r = 0; r < TILE_R; r++)
        for (int c = 0; c < TILE_C; c++)
            acc[r][c] = vec4(0.0);
    int tid = ly * WG_X + lx;   
    int numThreads = WG_X * WG_Y;  
    for (int kBase = 0; kBase < K; kBase += TILE_K)
    {
        
        
        
        for (int idx = tid; idx < TS_M * TILE_K; idx += numThreads)
        {
            int row = idx / TILE_K;      
            int col = idx - row * TILE_K; 
            int globalRow = wg_row + row;
            int globalK   = kBase + col;
            if (globalRow < oH && globalK < K)
                tileA[idx] = texelFetch(uInput0, ivec3(globalK, globalRow, zA), 0);
            else
                tileA[idx] = vec4(0.0);
        }
        
        
        for (int idx = tid; idx < TILE_K * TS_N; idx += numThreads)
        {
            int row = idx / TS_N;        
            int col = idx - row * TS_N;  
            int globalK   = kBase + row;
            int globalCol = wg_col + col;
            if (globalK < K && globalCol < oW)
            {
                vec4 val = texelFetch(uInput1, ivec3(globalCol, globalK, zB), 0);
#ifdef CHWxHW
                val = vec4(val.x);  
#endif
                tileB[idx] = val;
            }
            else
                tileB[idx] = vec4(0.0);
        }
        memoryBarrierShared();
        barrier();
        
        for (int k = 0; k < TILE_K; k++)
        {
            
            vec4 a[TILE_R];
            for (int r = 0; r < TILE_R; r++)
                a[r] = tileA[(ly * TILE_R + r) * TILE_K + k];
            
            vec4 b[TILE_C];
            for (int c = 0; c < TILE_C; c++)
                b[c] = tileB[k * TS_N + lx * TILE_C + c];
            
            for (int r = 0; r < TILE_R; r++)
                for (int c = 0; c < TILE_C; c++)
                    acc[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
        }
        barrier();
    }
    for (int r = 0; r < TILE_R; r++)
    {
        int oy = wg_row + ly * TILE_R + r;
        if (oy >= oH) continue;
        for (int c = 0; c < TILE_C; c++)
        {
            int ox = wg_col + lx * TILE_C + c;
            if (ox >= oW) continue;
            imageStore(uOutput, ivec3(ox, oy, gz), acc[r][c]);
        }
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(location=0) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(FORMAT, binding=1) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
layout(binding = 1) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
#endif
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec2 uKernel;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uStride;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uPad;
layout(location = 10) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 11) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    ivec3 outputSize = uOutputSize;
    ivec2 spos = pos.xy * uStride - uPad;
#ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (all(lessThan(pos, outputSize)))
#endif
    {
        ivec3 inputSize = uInputSize;
        ivec2 sfxy = max(ivec2(0), -spos);
        ivec2 efxy = min(uKernel, inputSize.xy - spos);
        vec4 color = vec4(-100000.0);
        for (int fy = sfxy.y; fy < efxy.y; ++fy)
        {
            for (int fx = sfxy.x; fx < efxy.x; ++fx)
            {
                #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                color = max(color, texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(spos.x + fx, spos.y + fy, pos.z), 0));
                #else
                vec4 in_color = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*(spos.y + fy) + (spos.x + fx) + pos.z*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
                color = max(color, in_color);
                #endif
            }
        }
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + pos.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y] = color;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uImage;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
#endif
layout(binding=1) readonly buffer destBuffer{
    float data[];
} uInBuffer;
layout(location = 2) uniform int uWidth;
layout(location = 3) uniform int uHeight;
layout(location = 4) uniform int uChannel;
layout(location = 5) uniform int uBatch;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uTile;
layout (local_size_x = 8, local_size_y = 8, local_size_z = 1) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
#ifdef MULTI_BATCH
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (pos.x < uTile.x * uWidth && pos.y < uTile.y * uHeight)
    #endif
    {
        
        int w = pos.x % uWidth;
        int h = pos.y % uHeight;
        int c = pos.z * 4;
        int b = (pos.y / uHeight) * uTile.x + (pos.x / uWidth);
        vec4 color;
        color.r = (c + 0) < uChannel ? uInBuffer.data[w + uWidth * h + (c + 0) * uWidth * uHeight + b * uWidth * uHeight * uChannel] : float(0);
        color.g = (c + 1) < uChannel ? uInBuffer.data[w + uWidth * h + (c + 1) * uWidth * uHeight + b * uWidth * uHeight * uChannel] : float(0);
        color.b = (c + 2) < uChannel ? uInBuffer.data[w + uWidth * h + (c + 2) * uWidth * uHeight + b * uWidth * uHeight * uChannel] : float(0);
        color.a = (c + 3) < uChannel ? uInBuffer.data[w + uWidth * h + (c + 3) * uWidth * uHeight + b * uWidth * uHeight * uChannel] : float(0);
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uImage, pos, color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[uWidth * uBatch * pos.y + pos.x + pos.z * uWidth * uBatch * uHeight] = color;
        #endif
    }
#else
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (pos.x < uWidth && pos.y < uHeight)
    #endif
    {
        vec4 color;
        int z = pos.z*4;
        color.r = uInBuffer.data[uWidth*pos.y+pos.x+(z+0)*uWidth*uHeight];
        color.g = uInBuffer.data[uWidth*pos.y+pos.x+(z+1)*uWidth*uHeight];
        color.b = uInBuffer.data[uWidth*pos.y+pos.x+(z+2)*uWidth*uHeight];
        color.a = uInBuffer.data[uWidth*pos.y+pos.x+(z+3)*uWidth*uHeight];
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uImage, pos, color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x + pos.z * uWidth * uHeight] = color;
        #endif
    }
#endif
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput0{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(location = 2) uniform ivec4 uInputSize;
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec4 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec4 padParams;
layout(location = 5) uniform float padValue;
layout(location = 6) uniform int input_channel;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uOutputTile;
layout(location = 8) uniform ivec2 uInputTile;
#define COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos , w, h, tileW) ivec4(pos[0]%w, pos[1]%h, pos[2], pos[0]/w + (pos[1]/h)*tileW)
#define COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, w, h, tileW) ivec3(pos4[0] + (pos4[3]%tileW)*w, pos4[1] + (pos4[3]/tileW)*h, pos4[2])
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    ivec4 pos4 = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos,uOutputSize.x,uOutputSize.y,uOutputTile.x);
    int input_w_idx = 0;
    int input_h_idx = 0;
    vec4 outValue;
    ivec4 in_pos;
    if(all(lessThan(pos4,uOutputSize)))
    {
        int input_w_idx = pos4.x - padParams.x;
        int input_h_idx = pos4.y - padParams.y;
        int input_b_idx = pos4.w - padParams.w;
        int remain      = padParams.z % 4;
        int input_c_b_idx               = pos4.z * 4 - padParams.z;
        int input_c_e_idx               = input_c_b_idx + 3;
        int input_channel_b_block_idx   = pos4.z - (padParams.z + 3) / 4;
        if (input_b_idx >= 0 && input_b_idx < uInputSize.w && input_w_idx >= 0 && input_w_idx < uInputSize.x && input_h_idx >= 0 &&
            input_h_idx < uInputSize.y && input_c_b_idx < input_channel && input_c_e_idx >= 0)
        {
            if (remain == 0)
            {
                in_pos.x = input_w_idx;
                in_pos.y = input_h_idx;
                in_pos.z = input_channel_b_block_idx;
                in_pos.w = input_b_idx;
                #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                outValue = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x), 0);
                #else
                ivec3 in3 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x);
                outValue = uInput.data[in3.x + in3.y * uInputSize.x + in3.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
                #endif
                int remain_channels = input_channel - input_c_b_idx;
                if (remain_channels <= 3)
                {
                    if (remain_channels == 3)
                    {
                        outValue.w = padValue;
                    }
                    else if (remain_channels == 2)
                    {
                        outValue.z = outValue.w = padValue;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        outValue.y = outValue.z = outValue.w = padValue;
                    }
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (input_c_b_idx >= 0 && input_c_e_idx < input_channel)
                {
                    in_pos.x = input_w_idx;
                    in_pos.y = input_h_idx;
                    in_pos.z = input_channel_b_block_idx;
                    in_pos.w = input_b_idx;
                    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                    vec4 temp_b_value = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x), 0);
                    #else
                    ivec3 in3b = COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x);
                    vec4 temp_b_value = uInput.data[in3b.x + in3b.y * uInputSize.x + in3b.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
                    #endif
                    in_pos.x = input_w_idx;
                    in_pos.y = input_h_idx;
                    in_pos.z = input_channel_b_block_idx + 1;
                    in_pos.w = input_b_idx;
                    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                    vec4 temp_e_value = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x), 0);
                    #else
                    ivec3 in3e = COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x);
                    vec4 temp_e_value = uInput.data[in3e.x + in3e.y * uInputSize.x + in3e.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
                    #endif
                    outValue = vec4(padValue);
                    if (remain == 1)
                    {
                        outValue.x = temp_b_value.w;
                        outValue.yzw = temp_e_value.xyz;
                    }
                    else if (remain == 2)
                    {
                        outValue.xy = temp_b_value.zw;
                        outValue.zw = temp_e_value.xy;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        outValue.xyz = temp_b_value.yzw;
                        outValue.w = temp_e_value.x;
                    }
                }
                else if (input_c_b_idx < 0)
                {
                    in_pos.x = input_w_idx;
                    in_pos.y = input_h_idx;
                    in_pos.z = 0;
                    in_pos.w = input_b_idx;
                    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                    vec4 temp_value = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x), 0);
                    #else
                    ivec3 in3v = COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x);
                    vec4 temp_value = uInput.data[in3v.x + in3v.y * uInputSize.x + in3v.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
                    #endif
                    outValue = vec4(padValue);
                    if (remain == 1)
                    {
                        if (input_channel >= 3)
                        {
                            outValue.yzw = temp_value.xyz;
                        }
                        else if (input_channel == 2)
                        {
                            outValue.yz = temp_value.xy;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            outValue.y = temp_value.x;
                        }
                    }
                    else if (remain == 2)
                    {
                        if (input_channel >= 2)
                        {
                            outValue.zw = temp_value.xy;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            outValue.z = temp_value.x;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        outValue.w = temp_value.x;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    in_pos.x = input_w_idx;
                    in_pos.y = input_h_idx;
                    in_pos.z = input_channel_b_block_idx;
                    in_pos.w = input_b_idx;
                    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                    vec4 temp_b_value = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x), 0);
                    #else
                    ivec3 in3b2 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x);
                    vec4 temp_b_value = uInput.data[in3b2.x + in3b2.y * uInputSize.x + in3b2.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
                    #endif
                    in_pos.x = input_w_idx;
                    in_pos.y = input_h_idx;
                    in_pos.z = input_channel_b_block_idx + 1;
                    in_pos.w = input_b_idx;
                    #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
                    vec4 temp_e_value = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x), 0);
                    #else
                    ivec3 in3e2 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(in_pos,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x);
                    vec4 temp_e_value = uInput.data[in3e2.x + in3e2.y * uInputSize.x + in3e2.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
                    #endif
                    outValue = vec4(padValue);
                    int remain_channels = input_channel - input_c_b_idx;
                    if (remain == 1)
                    {
                        if (remain_channels == 3)
                        {
                            outValue.x = temp_b_value.w;
                            outValue.yz = temp_e_value.xy;
                        }
                        else if (remain_channels == 2)
                        {
                            outValue.x = temp_b_value.w;
                            outValue.y = temp_e_value.x;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            outValue.x = temp_b_value.w;
                        }
                    }
                    else if (remain == 2)
                    {
                        if (remain_channels == 3)
                        {
                            outValue.xy = temp_b_value.zw;
                            outValue.z = temp_e_value.x;
                        }
                        else if (remain_channels == 2)
                        {
                            outValue.xy = temp_b_value.zw;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            outValue.x = temp_b_value.z;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (remain_channels == 3)
                        {
                            outValue.xyz = temp_b_value.yzw;
                        }
                        else if (remain_channels == 2)
                        {
                            outValue.xy = temp_b_value.yz;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            outValue.x = temp_b_value.y;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        else
        {
            outValue = vec4(padValue);
        }
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, outValue);
        #else
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = outValue;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput0{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(location = 2) uniform ivec4 uInputSize;
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec4 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec3 padParams;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uOutputTile;
layout(location = 8) uniform ivec2 uInputTile;
#define COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos , w, h, tileW) ivec4(pos[0]%w, pos[1]%h, pos[2], pos[0]/w + (pos[1]/h)*tileW)
#define COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, w, h, tileW) ivec3(pos4[0] + (pos4[3]%tileW)*w, pos4[1] + (pos4[3]/tileW)*h, pos4[2])
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    ivec4 pos4 = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos,uOutputSize.x,uOutputSize.y,uOutputTile.x);
    if(all(lessThan(pos4,uOutputSize)))
    {
        int h = pos4.y - padParams.y;
        int w = pos4.x - padParams.x;
        h = max(h,-h);
        w = max(w,-w);
        h = min(h, 2 * uInputSize.y - h - 2);
        w = min(w, 2 * uInputSize.x - w - 2);
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 outValue = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(ivec4(w,h,pos4.zw),uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x), 0);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, outValue);
        #else
        vec4 outValue = uInput.data[w + h * uInputSize.x + pos.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = outValue;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput0{
    float data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(location=2) uniform ivec3 uInShapeStride;
layout(location=3) uniform ivec4 uInputSize;
layout(location=4) uniform ivec4 uOutputSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
float getPixel(int w, int h, int c)
    int c4 = c / 4;
    int cr = c - c4 * 4;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
    vec4 pixel4 = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(w,h,c4), 0);
    return pixel4[cr];
#else
    int c4_idx = w + h * uInputSize.x + c4 * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y;
    return uInput.data[c4_idx * 4 + cr];
#endif
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if(pos.x < uOutputSize.x && pos.y < uOutputSize.y && pos.z < uOutputSize.z)
    {
        
        ivec4 outC = ivec4(pos.z * 4) + ivec4(0, 1, 2, 3);
        ivec4 outH = ivec4(pos.y);
        ivec4 outW = ivec4(pos.x);
        
        ivec4 inOffsetIdx = outC * uInShapeStride[0] + outH * uInShapeStride[1] + outW * uInShapeStride[2];
        
        ivec4 inC = inOffsetIdx / (uInputSize.y * uInputSize.x);
        inOffsetIdx -= inC * (uInputSize.y * uInputSize.x);
        ivec4 inH = inOffsetIdx / uInputSize.x;
        ivec4 inW = inOffsetIdx - inH * uInputSize.x;
        
        vec4 outValue = vec4(getPixel(inW.x, inH.x, inC.x),
                            getPixel(inW.y, inH.y, inC.y),
                            getPixel(inW.z, inH.z, inC.z),
                            getPixel(inW.w, inH.w, inC.w));
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, outValue);
        #else
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = outValue;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(binding=2) readonly buffer slope{
    vec4 data[];
} uSlope;
layout(location=3) uniform ivec3 imgSize;
layout(location=4) uniform ivec3 outSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
#ifndef NO_BOUND
    if(all(lessThan(pos, imgSize)))
#endif
    {
        vec4 slope = uSlope.data[pos.z];
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 dataIn = texelFetch(uInput, pos, 0);
        vec4 dataTemp = dataIn * slope;
        bvec4 lessZero = bvec4(lessThan(dataIn, vec4(0.0)));
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, mix(dataIn, dataTemp, lessZero));
        #else
        vec4 dataIn = uInput.data[ imgSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + pos.z*imgSize.x*imgSize.y ];
        vec4 dataTemp = dataIn * slope;
        bvec4 lessZero = bvec4(lessThan(dataIn, vec4(0.0)));
        uOutput.data[outSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + pos.z*outSize.x*outSize.y] = mix(dataIn, dataTemp, lessZero);
        #endif
    }
#ifndef NO_KEEPDIMS
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
#else
layout(binding=0) writeonly buffer destBuffer{
    float data[];
} uOutput;
#endif
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(location = 2) uniform ivec4 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec4 uInputSize;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec2 uOutputTile;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uInputTile;
layout(location = 6) uniform float uReduceSize;
#define COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos , w, h, tileW) ivec4(pos[0]%w, pos[1]%h, pos[2], pos[0]/w + (pos[1]/h)*tileW)
#define COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, w, h, tileW) ivec3(pos4[0] + (pos4[3]%tileW)*w, pos4[1] + (pos4[3]/tileW)*h, pos4[2])
const float FLOAT_MAX = 1.70141184e38;
const float FLOAT_MIN = -FLOAT_MAX;
layout (local_size_x = 8, local_size_y = 8, local_size_z = 1) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (pos.x < uOutputTile.x * uOutputSize.x && pos.y < uOutputTile.y * uOutputSize.y)
    {
        ivec4 pos4 = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos, uOutputSize.x, uOutputSize.y, uOutputTile.x);
        ivec4 tmp_pos4 = pos4;
        vec4 ResultData = vec4(0);
        #ifdef OPERATION_MAX
            ResultData = vec4(FLOAT_MIN);
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_MIN
            ResultData = vec4(FLOAT_MAX);
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_PROD
            ResultData = vec4(1);
        #endif
        #ifdef REDUCE_BATCH
        for(int b = 0; b < uInputSize[3]; b++)
        {
            tmp_pos4[3] = b;
        #endif
            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
            for(int c4 = 0; c4 < (uInputSize[2]+3)/4; c4++)
            {
                tmp_pos4[2] = c4;
            #endif
                #ifdef REDUCE_HEIGHT
                for(int h = 0; h < uInputSize[1]; h++)
                {
                    tmp_pos4[1] = h;
                #endif
                    #ifdef REDUCE_WIDTH
                    for(int w = 0; w < uInputSize[0]; w++)
                    {
                        tmp_pos4[0] = w;
                    #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_MEAN
                        ResultData += texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0);
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_SUM
                        ResultData += texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0);
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_MAX
                        ResultData = max(ResultData, texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0));
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_MIN
                        vec4 data = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0);
                            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
                                int c = tmp_pos4[2] * 4;
                                data[0] = (c + 0) < uInputSize.z ? data[0] : FLOAT_MAX;
                                data[1] = (c + 1) < uInputSize.z ? data[1] : FLOAT_MAX;
                                data[2] = (c + 2) < uInputSize.z ? data[2] : FLOAT_MAX;
                                data[3] = (c + 3) < uInputSize.z ? data[3] : FLOAT_MAX;
                            #endif
                        ResultData = min(ResultData, data);
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_PROD
                        vec4 data = texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0);
                            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
                                int c = tmp_pos4[2] * 4;
                                data[0] =  (c + 0) < uInputSize.z ? data[0] : float(1);
                                data[1] =  (c + 1) < uInputSize.z ? data[1] : float(1);
                                data[2] =  (c + 2) < uInputSize.z ? data[2] : float(1);
                                data[3] =  (c + 3) < uInputSize.z ? data[3] : float(1);
                            #endif
                        ResultData *= data;
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_SUMSQ
                        ResultData += pow(texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0), vec4(2));
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_L1
                        ResultData += abs(texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0));
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_L2
                        ResultData += pow(texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0), vec4(2));
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_LOGSUM
                        ResultData += texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0);
                        #endif
                        #ifdef OPERATION_LOGSUMEXP
                        ResultData += exp(texelFetch(uInput, COORDTRANS_4TO3(tmp_pos4, uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputSize.x), 0));
                        #endif
                    #ifdef REDUCE_WIDTH
                    }
                    #endif
                #ifdef REDUCE_HEIGHT
                }
                #endif
            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
            }
            #endif
        #ifdef REDUCE_BATCH
        }
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_MEAN
            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
                float fsum = ResultData[0] + ResultData[1] + ResultData[2] + ResultData[3];
                ResultData = vec4(fsum/uReduceSize);
            #else
                ResultData = ResultData/uReduceSize;
            #endif
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_SUM
            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
                float fsum = ResultData[0] + ResultData[1] + ResultData[2] + ResultData[3];
                ResultData = vec4(fsum);
            #endif
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_MAX
            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
                float fmax = max(max(max(ResultData[0], ResultData[1]), ResultData[2]), ResultData[3]);
                ResultData = vec4(fmax);
            #endif
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_MIN
            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
                float fmin = min(min(min(ResultData[0], ResultData[1]), ResultData[2]), ResultData[3]);
                ResultData = vec4(fmin);
            #endif
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_PROD
            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
                float fprod = ResultData[0] * ResultData[1] * ResultData[2] * ResultData[3];
                ResultData = vec4(fprod);
            #endif
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_SUMSQ
            #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
                float fsumsq = pow(ResultData[0],2) + pow(ResultData[1],2) + pow(ResultData[2],2) * pow(ResultData[3],2);
                ResultData = vec4(fsumsq);
            #endif
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_L1
        #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
            float fsum = ResultData[0] + ResultData[1] + ResultData[2] + ResultData[3];
            ResultData = vec4(fsum);
        #endif
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_L2
        #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
            float fsum = ResultData[0] + ResultData[1] + ResultData[2] + ResultData[3];
            ResultData = vec4(fsum);
        #endif
        ResultData = sqrt(ResultData);
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_LOGSUM
        #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
            float fsum = ResultData[0] + ResultData[1] + ResultData[2] + ResultData[3];
            ResultData = vec4(fsum);
        #endif
        ResultData=log(ResultData);
        #endif
        #ifdef OPERATION_LOGSUMEXP
        #ifdef REDUCE_CHANNEL
            float fsum = ResultData[0] + ResultData[1] + ResultData[2] + ResultData[3];
            ResultData = vec4(fsum);
        #endif
        ResultData=log(ResultData);
        #endif
        #ifndef NO_KEEPDIMS
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, ResultData);
        #else
        int z = pos4.z * 4;
        if(z+3 < uOutputSize.z){
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 0) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.r;
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 1) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.g;
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 2) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.b;
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 3) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.a;
        }else if(z+2 < uOutputSize.z){
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 0) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.r;
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 1) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.g;
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 2) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.b;
        }else if(z+1 < uOutputSize.z){
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 0) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.r;
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 1) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.g;
        }else if(z < uOutputSize.z){
            uOutput.data[pos4.x + pos4.y * uOutputSize.x + (z + 0) * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y + pos4.w * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y * uOutputSize.z] = ResultData.r;
        }
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;

#else

} uOutput;

layout(location = 2) uniform ivec3 imgSize;
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec3 outSize;
layout(location = 4) uniform float uSlope;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
#ifndef NO_BOUND
    if (all(lessThan(pos, imgSize)))
#endif
    {
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 dataIn = texelFetch(uInput, pos, 0);
        bvec4 lessZero = bvec4(lessThan(dataIn, vec4(0.0)));
        vec4 dataTemp = dataIn * vec4(uSlope);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, mix(dataIn, dataTemp, lessZero));
#else
        vec4 dataIn = uInput.data[imgSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + pos.z*imgSize.x*imgSize.y];
        bvec4 lessZero = bvec4(lessThan(dataIn, vec4(0.0)));
        vec4 dataTemp = dataIn * vec4(uSlope);
        uOutput.data[outSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + pos.z*outSize.x*outSize.y] = mix(dataIn, dataTemp, lessZero);
#endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput0;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput0;
#endif
layout(location = 2) uniform ivec4 inImgSize;
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec4 outImgSize;
layout(location = 4) uniform vec2 scale;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if(all(lessThan(pos, outImgSize.xyz)))
    #endif
    {
#ifdef PYTORCH_HALF_PIXEL
        float srcX = max(0.f, (float(pos.x) + 0.5f) * scale.x - 0.5f);
        float srcY = max(0.f, (float(pos.y) + 0.5f) * scale.y - 0.5f);
#else
        float srcX = float(pos.x) * scale.x;
        float srcY = float(pos.y) * scale.y;
#endif
        int x1 = int(floor(srcX));
        int x11 = clamp(x1, 0, inImgSize.x - 1);
        int x12 = clamp(x1 + 1, 0, inImgSize.x - 1);
        vec4 factorX = vec4(srcX - float(x1));
        int y1 = int(floor(srcY));
        int y11 = clamp(y1, 0, inImgSize.y - 1);
        int y12 = clamp(y1 + 1, 0, inImgSize.y - 1);
        vec4 factorY = vec4(srcY - float(y1));
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 res1 = texelFetch(uInput0, ivec3(x11, y12, pos.z), 0);
        vec4 res2 = texelFetch(uInput0, ivec3(x12, y12, pos.z), 0);
        vec4 res3 = texelFetch(uInput0, ivec3(x11, y11, pos.z), 0);
        vec4 res4 = texelFetch(uInput0, ivec3(x12, y11, pos.z), 0);
        #else
        vec4 res1 = uInput0.data[ inImgSize.x*(y12) + (x11) + pos.z*inImgSize.x*inImgSize.y ];
        vec4 res2 = uInput0.data[ inImgSize.x*(y12) + (x12) + pos.z*inImgSize.x*inImgSize.y ];
        vec4 res3 = uInput0.data[ inImgSize.x*(y11) + (x11) + pos.z*inImgSize.x*inImgSize.y ];
        vec4 res4 = uInput0.data[ inImgSize.x*(y11) + (x12) + pos.z*inImgSize.x*inImgSize.y ];
        #endif
        vec4 res11 = (vec4(1.0) - factorX) * res1 + factorX * res2;
        vec4 res12 = (vec4(1.0) - factorX) * res3 + factorX * res4;
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, factorY * res11 + (vec4(1.0) - factorY) * res12);
        #else
        uOutput.data[outImgSize.x*pos.y  + pos.x + pos.z*outImgSize.x*outImgSize.y] = factorY * res11 + (vec4(1.0) - factorY) * res12;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else

} uOutput;

} uInput;

layout(location=2) uniform ivec4 inImgSize;
layout(location=3) uniform ivec4 outImgSize;
layout(location=4) uniform vec2 scale;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    ivec3 inputImgSize = inImgSize.xyz;
    ivec3 outputImgSize = outImgSize.xyz;
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if(pos.x < outputImgSize.x && pos.y < outputImgSize.y)
    #endif
    {
        float srcX = float(pos.x) * scale.x;
        int x1 = int(floor(srcX));
        int x11 = clamp(x1, 0, inputImgSize.x - 1);
        float srcY = float(pos.y) * scale.y;
        int y1 = int(floor(srcY));
        int y11 = clamp(y1, 0, inputImgSize.y - 1);
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 outValue = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(x11, y11, pos.z), 0);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, outValue);
        #else
        vec4 outValue = uInput.data[ inImgSize.x*(y11) + (x11) + pos.z*inImgSize.x*inImgSize.y ];
        uOutput.data[outImgSize.x*pos.y  + pos.x + pos.z*outImgSize.x*outImgSize.y] = outValue;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput0;
#else

} uOutput;

    vec4 data[];
} uInput0;
#endif
#ifdef STATIC_SCALE
layout(binding = 2) readonly buffer scale{
    vec4 data[];
} uScale;
#else
layout(location = 2) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput1;
#endif
#ifdef BIAS
layout(binding = 3) readonly buffer bias{
    vec4 data[];
} uBias;
#endif
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec3 imgSize;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec3 outSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    #ifndef NO_BOUND
    if(all(lessThan(pos, imgSize)))
    #endif
    {
        #ifdef BIAS
        vec4 color = uBias.data[pos.z];
        #else
        vec4 color  = vec4(0);
        #endif
        #ifdef STATIC_SCALE
        vec4 alpha  = uScale.data[pos.z];
        #endif
        #ifdef DYNAMIC_SCALE_CHANNEL
        vec4 alpha  = texelFetch(uInput1, ivec3(0,0,pos.z),0);
        #endif
        #ifdef DYNAMIC_SCALE_ONE
        vec4 alpha_ = texelFetch(uInput1, ivec3(0,0,0),0);
        vec4 alpha  = vec4(alpha_.x);
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        color   += texelFetch(uInput0, pos, 0) * alpha;
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, color);
        #else
        color   += uInput0.data[pos.x + imgSize.x*pos.y + pos.z*imgSize.x*imgSize.y] * alpha;
        uOutput.data[pos.x + outSize.x*pos.y + pos.z*outSize.x*outSize.y] =  color;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(location = 0) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(FORMAT, binding = 1) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) readonly buffer sinput0{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
layout(binding = 1) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;

layout(location = 3) uniform ivec4 uInputSize;
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec4 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uInputTile;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uOutputTile;
layout(location = 7) uniform int uIcOffset;
layout(location = 8) uniform ivec4 uInputOffset;
#define COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos , w, h, tileW) ivec4(pos[0]%w, pos[1]%h, pos[2], pos[0]/w + (pos[1]/h)*tileW)
#define COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, w, h, tileW) ivec3(pos4[0] + (pos4[3]%tileW)*w, pos4[1] + (pos4[3]/tileW)*h, pos4[2])
layout (local_size_x = 4, local_size_y = 4, local_size_z = 4) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (all(lessThan(pos, ivec3(uOutputSize.xy * uOutputTile, uOutputSize.z))))
    {
        ivec4 pos4 = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos,uOutputSize.x,uOutputSize.y,uOutputTile.x);
        ivec4 srcp4_0 = pos4 + uInputOffset / ivec4(1, 1, 4, 1);
        ivec3 srcp3_0 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(srcp4_0,uInputSize.x,uInputSize.y,uInputTile.x);
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 in0 = texelFetch(uInput, srcp3_0, 0);
        #else
        vec4 in0 = uInput.data[srcp3_0.x + srcp3_0.y * uInputSize.x + srcp3_0.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
        #endif
        #ifdef SLICE_CHANNEL
        if(uIcOffset > 0) {
            ivec3 srcp3_1 = srcp3_0 + ivec3(0, 0, 1);
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 in1 = texelFetch(uInput, srcp3_1,0);
            #else
            vec4 in1 = uInput.data[srcp3_1.x + srcp3_1.y * uInputSize.x + srcp3_1.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
            #endif
            for(int i = 0; i < 4 - uIcOffset; i++) {
                in0[i] = in0[i + uIcOffset];
            }
            for(int i = 0; i < uIcOffset; i++) {
                in0[i + 4 - uIcOffset] = in1[i];
            }
        }
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, in0);
        #else
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = in0;
        #endif
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else

} uOutput;
