layout(location = 2) uniform int w;
layout(location = 3) uniform int h;
layout(location = 4) uniform int c;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uTile;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
#define c_exp_hi 88.3762626647949
#define c_exp_lo -88.3762626647949
vec4 exp_clamp(vec4 data) {
#ifdef MEDIUMP
    data = clamp(data, -11.089, 11.089);
#else
    data = clamp(data, c_exp_lo, c_exp_hi);
#endif
    return data;
vec4 exp_safe(vec4 data) {
    return exp(exp_clamp(data));
float exp_clamp1(float data) {
#ifdef MEDIUMP
    data = clamp(data, -11.089, 11.089);
#else
    data = clamp(data, c_exp_lo, c_exp_hi);
#endif
    return data;
float exp_safe1(float data) {
    return exp(exp_clamp1(data));
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
#ifndef NO_BOUND
    if(pos.x < uTile.x * w && pos.y < uTile.y * h)
#endif
    {
        int channelDiv4 = c / 4;
        int upDiv4 = (c + 3) / 4;
        int lastChannel = c % 4;
        int batchIndex = pos.z * upDiv4;
        
        vec4 maxValue = vec4(-1000.0);
        for(int i = 0; i < channelDiv4; ++i)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            maxValue = max(maxValue, texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, i + batchIndex), 0));
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + (i + batchIndex)*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            maxValue = max(maxValue, tempData);
            #endif
        }
        float maxValueTrue = -1000.0;
        maxValueTrue = max(maxValue.x, maxValue.y);
        maxValueTrue = max(maxValueTrue, maxValue.z);
        maxValueTrue = max(maxValueTrue, maxValue.w);
        if(lastChannel == 1)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 tempData = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, channelDiv4 + batchIndex), 0);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + (channelDiv4 + batchIndex)*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            #endif
            maxValueTrue = max(maxValueTrue, tempData.x);
        }
        else if(lastChannel == 2)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 tempData = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, channelDiv4 + batchIndex), 0);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + (channelDiv4 + batchIndex)*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            #endif
            maxValueTrue = max(maxValueTrue, tempData.x);
            maxValueTrue = max(maxValueTrue, tempData.y);
        }
        else if(lastChannel == 3)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 tempData = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, channelDiv4 + batchIndex), 0);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + (channelDiv4 + batchIndex)*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            #endif
            maxValueTrue = max(maxValueTrue, tempData.x);
            maxValueTrue = max(maxValueTrue, tempData.y);
            maxValueTrue = max(maxValueTrue, tempData.z);
        }
        
        maxValue = vec4(maxValueTrue);
        vec4 sum = vec4(0.0);
        for(int i = 0; i < channelDiv4; ++i)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            sum += exp_safe(texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, i + batchIndex), 0) - maxValue);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + (i + batchIndex)*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            sum += exp_safe(tempData - maxValue);
            #endif
        }
        float sumTrue = 0.0;
        sumTrue = sum.x + sum.y + sum.z + sum.w;
        if(lastChannel == 1)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 tempData = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, channelDiv4 + batchIndex), 0);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + (channelDiv4 + batchIndex)*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            #endif
            sumTrue += exp_safe1(tempData.x - maxValueTrue);
        }
        else if(lastChannel == 2)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 tempData = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, channelDiv4 + batchIndex), 0);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + (channelDiv4 + batchIndex)*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            #endif
            sumTrue += (exp_safe1(tempData.x - maxValueTrue) + exp_safe1(tempData.y - maxValueTrue));
        }
        else if(lastChannel == 3)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            vec4 tempData = texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, channelDiv4 + batchIndex), 0);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*pos.y + pos.x + (channelDiv4 + batchIndex)*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            #endif
            sumTrue += (exp_safe1(tempData.x - maxValueTrue) + exp_safe1(tempData.y - maxValueTrue) + exp_safe1(tempData.z - maxValueTrue));
        }
        
        sum = vec4(sumTrue);
        for(int i = 0; i < upDiv4; ++i)
        {
            ivec3 curPos = ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, i + batchIndex);
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            imageStore(uOutput, curPos, exp_safe(texelFetch(uInput, curPos, 0) - maxValue) / sum);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[uInputSize.x*curPos.y + curPos.x + curPos.z*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            uOutput.data[uOutputSize.x*curPos.y + curPos.x + curPos.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y] = exp_safe(tempData - maxValue) / sum;
            #endif
        }
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(location=2) uniform int w;
layout(location=3) uniform int h;
layout(location=4) uniform int c;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uTile;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
#define c_exp_hi 88.3762626647949
#define c_exp_lo -88.3762626647949
vec4 exp_clamp(vec4 data) {
#ifdef MEDIUMP
    data = clamp(data, -11.089, 11.089);
#else
    data = clamp(data, c_exp_lo, c_exp_hi);
#endif
    return data;
vec4 exp_safe(vec4 data) {
    return exp(exp_clamp(data));
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    int channelDiv4 = (c + 3) / 4;
    if(pos.x < uTile.x * w && pos.z < channelDiv4)
    {
        int HeightIndex = pos.y * h;
        
        vec4 maxValue = vec4(-1000.0);
        for(int i = HeightIndex; i < HeightIndex + h; ++i)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            maxValue = max(maxValue, texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, i, pos.z), 0));
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[ uInputSize.x*i + pos.x + pos.z*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            maxValue = max(maxValue, tempData);
            #endif
        }
        
        vec4 sum = vec4(0.0);
        for(int i = HeightIndex; i < HeightIndex + h; ++i)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            sum += exp_safe(texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(pos.x, i, pos.z), 0) - maxValue);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[ uInputSize.x*i + pos.x + pos.z*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            sum += exp_safe(tempData - maxValue);
            #endif
        }
        
        for(int i = HeightIndex; i < HeightIndex + h; ++i)
        {
            ivec3 curPos = ivec3(pos.x, i, pos.z);
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            imageStore(uOutput, curPos, exp_safe(texelFetch(uInput, curPos, 0) - maxValue) / sum);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[ uInputSize.x*curPos.y + curPos.x + curPos.z*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            uOutput.data[ uOutputSize.x*curPos.y + curPos.x + curPos.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y ] = exp_safe(tempData - maxValue) / sum;
            #endif
        }
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(location=2) uniform int w;
layout(location=3) uniform int h;
layout(location=4) uniform int c;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec2 uTile;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
#define c_exp_hi 88.3762626647949
#define c_exp_lo -88.3762626647949
vec4 exp_clamp(vec4 data) {
#ifdef MEDIUMP
    data = clamp(data, -11.089, 11.089);
#else
    data = clamp(data, c_exp_lo, c_exp_hi);
#endif
    return data;
vec4 exp_safe(vec4 data) {
    return exp(exp_clamp(data));
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    int channelDiv4 = (c + 3) / 4;
    if(pos.y < uTile.y * h && pos.z < channelDiv4)
    {
        int WeightIndex = pos.x * w;
        
        vec4 maxValue = vec4(-1000.0);
        for(int i = WeightIndex; i < WeightIndex + w; ++i)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            maxValue = max(maxValue, texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(i, pos.y, pos.z), 0));
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[ uInputSize.x*pos.y + i + pos.z*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            maxValue = max(maxValue, tempData);
            #endif
        }
        
        vec4 sum = vec4(0.0);
        for(int i = WeightIndex; i < WeightIndex + w; ++i)
        {
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            sum += exp_safe(texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(i, pos.y, pos.z), 0) - maxValue);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[ uInputSize.x*pos.y + i + pos.z*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            sum += exp_safe(tempData - maxValue);
            #endif
        }
        
        for(int i = WeightIndex; i < WeightIndex + w; ++i)
        {
            ivec3 curPos = ivec3(i, pos.y, pos.z);
            #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
            imageStore(uOutput, curPos, exp_safe(texelFetch(uInput, ivec3(i, pos.y, pos.z), 0) - maxValue) / sum);
            #else
            vec4 tempData = uInput.data[ uInputSize.x*pos.y + i + pos.z*uInputSize.x*uInputSize.y];
            uOutput.data[ uOutputSize.x*curPos.y + curPos.x + curPos.z*uOutputSize.x*uOutputSize.y ] = exp_safe(tempData - maxValue) / sum;
            #endif
        }
    }
layout(location = 0) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
layout(FORMAT, binding = 1) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec4 uInputSize; 
layout(location = 4) uniform ivec4 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 5) uniform ivec2 uInputTile;
layout(location = 6) uniform ivec2 uOutputTile;
layout(location = 7) uniform ivec4 uStarts;
layout(location = 8) uniform ivec4 uSteps;
#ifdef SLICE_CHANNEL
float getValueFromCoord(ivec3 pos, int idx)
    vec4 data = texelFetch(uInput, pos,0);
    return data[idx];
#endif
#define COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos , w, h, tileW) ivec4(pos[0]%w, pos[1]%h, pos[2], pos[0]/w + (pos[1]/h)*tileW)
#define COORDTRANS_4TO3(pos4, w, h, tileW) ivec3(pos4[0] + (pos4[3]%tileW)*w, pos4[1] + (pos4[3]/tileW)*h, pos4[2])
layout (local_size_x = 4, local_size_y = 4, local_size_z = 4) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    ivec4 pos4 = COORDTRANS_3TO4(pos,uOutputSize.x,uOutputSize.y,uOutputTile.x);
    if (all(lessThan(pos4, uOutputSize)))
    {
        int W_Idx = uStarts[0] + pos4[0] * uSteps[0];
        int H_Idx = uStarts[1] + pos4[1] * uSteps[1];
        int B_Idx = uStarts[3] + pos4[3] * uSteps[3];
        #ifdef SLICE_CHANNEL
        int C_Idx = uStarts[2] +  4 * uSteps[2] * pos4[2] ;
        ivec3 pos3 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(ivec4(W_Idx,H_Idx,C_Idx/4,B_Idx), uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputTile.x);
        float d0 = getValueFromCoord(pos3, C_Idx%4);
        C_Idx += uSteps[2];
        pos3 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(ivec4(W_Idx,H_Idx,C_Idx/4,B_Idx), uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputTile.x);
        float d1 = getValueFromCoord(pos3, C_Idx%4);
        C_Idx += uSteps[2];
        pos3 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(ivec4(W_Idx,H_Idx,C_Idx/4,B_Idx), uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputTile.x);
        float d2 = getValueFromCoord(pos3, C_Idx%4);
        C_Idx += uSteps[2];
        pos3 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(ivec4(W_Idx,H_Idx,C_Idx/4,B_Idx), uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputTile.x);
        float d3 = getValueFromCoord(pos3, C_Idx%4);
        vec4 data = vec4(d0,d1,d2,d3);
        #else
        ivec3 pos3 = COORDTRANS_4TO3(ivec4(W_Idx,H_Idx,pos4[2],B_Idx), uInputSize.x, uInputSize.y, uInputTile.x);
        vec4 data = texelFetch(uInput, pos3,0);
        #endif
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, data);
    }
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uImage;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
#endif
uniform sampler2D uInput2D;
layout(location = 2) uniform int uWidth;
layout(location = 3) uniform int uHeight;
layout(location = 4) uniform vec4 uScale;
layout (local_size_x = 8, local_size_y = 8, local_size_z = 1) in;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if (pos.x >= uWidth || pos.y >= uHeight) return;
    vec4 color = texelFetch(uInput2D, ivec2(pos.x, pos.y), 0);
    color *= uScale;
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
    imageStore(uImage, ivec3(pos.x, pos.y, 0), color);
#else
    uOutput.data[uWidth * pos.y + pos.x] = color;
#endif
#if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
layout(FORMAT, binding = 0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location = 1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    float data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput0{
    float data[];
} uInput;
#endif
layout(location = 2) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout(location = 3) uniform ivec3 uInputSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
void main() {
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if(all(lessThan(pos, uOutputSize))) {
        ivec3 newPos = pos - pos / uInputSize * uInputSize;
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 color = texelFetch(uInput, newPos, 0);
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, color);
        #else
        float color = uInput.data[(newPos.x + newPos.y * uInputSize.x + newPos.z / 4 * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y) * 4 + newPos.z % 4];
        uOutput.data[(pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z / 4 * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y) * 4 + pos.z % 4] = color;
        #endif
    }

layout(FORMAT, binding=0) writeonly uniform PRECISION image3D uOutput;
layout(location=1) uniform PRECISION sampler3D uInput;
#else
layout(binding = 0) writeonly buffer soutput{
    vec4 data[];
} uOutput;
layout(binding = 1) readonly buffer sinput0{
    vec4 data[];
} uInput;

layout(location=3) uniform ivec3 uOutputSize;
layout (local_size_x = XLOCAL, local_size_y = YLOCAL, local_size_z = ZLOCAL) in;
vec4 sigmoid(vec4 dataIn) {
    vec4 color = 1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-dataIn));
    return color;
vec4 UnaryOpNot(vec4 dataIn) {
    vec4 zero = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    bvec4 eqret = equal(zero, dataIn);
    vec4 ret = vec4(eqret.xyzw);
    return ret;
#define c_exp_hi 88.3762626647949
#define c_exp_lo -88.3762626647949
vec4 exp_clamp(vec4 data) {
#ifdef MEDIUMP
    data = clamp(data, -11.089, 11.089);
#else
    data = clamp(data, c_exp_lo, c_exp_hi);
#endif
    return data;
void main()
    ivec3 pos = ivec3(gl_GlobalInvocationID);
    if(all(lessThan(pos, uOutputSize)))
    {
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        vec4 data = texelFetch(uInput, pos, 0);
        #else
        vec4 data = uInput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uInputSize.x + pos.z * uInputSize.x * uInputSize.y];
        #endif
        vec4 color = data;
        #ifdef EXP
        data = exp_clamp(data);
        color = exp(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef SIGMOID
        data = exp_clamp(data);
        color = sigmoid(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef TANH
        data = exp_clamp(data);
        color = tanh(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef FLOOR
        color = floor(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef NEG
        color = -(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef ABS
        color = abs(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef COS
        color = cos(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef SIN
        color = sin(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef SQRT
        color = sqrt(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef CEIL
        color = ceil(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef LOG
        color = log(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef TAN
        color = tan(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef ATAN
        color = atan(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef ASIN
        color = asin(data);
        #endif
        #ifdef NOT
        color = UnaryOpNot(data);
        #endif
        #if (PREFERED_TEXTURE)
        imageStore(uOutput, pos, color);
        #else
        uOutput.data[pos.x + pos.y * uOutputSize.x + pos.z * uOutputSize.x * uOutputSize.y] = color;
        #endif
    }
